FILE: CustomerApi\Controllers\AssetController.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Asset;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Infrastructure;
using Polly.Timeout;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/asset")]


public class AssetController : ControllerBase
{
    IAssetService _assetServ;


    public AssetController(IAssetService cs)
    {
        _assetServ = cs;
    }




    [HttpGet]
    public ActionResult<List<AssetResponseDto>> Get()
    {
        return Ok(_assetServ.Fetch());
    }


    [HttpGet("me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<Asset>>> GetMyAssets()
    {
        string? uId = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);
        Guid userId = Guid.Parse(uId);
        return Ok(await _assetServ.GetAssetsByUserId(userId));
    }


    [HttpGet("{id:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AssetFullDetailsDto>> Get(Guid id)
    {
        try
        {
            AssetFullDetailsDto c = await _assetServ.Fetch(id);


            if (c != null)
            {
                return Ok(c);
            }
        }
        catch (TimeoutRejectedException ex)
        {
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status408RequestTimeout,
                new
                {
                    Status = 504,
                    Message = "InsuranceService did not respond within the allowed time."
                });
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {


            return BadRequest(ex.Message);
        }
        return NotFound($"Asset with id: {id} Not Found");
    }


    [HttpGet("customer/{id:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<Asset>> GetByCustId(Guid id)
    {
        return Ok(_assetServ.FetchByCustId(id));
    }


    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<ActionResult> Post(AssetCreateDto assetInp)
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Recieved Request for Asset Post..", assetInp);
        AssetResponseDto res = await _assetServ.Create(assetInp);
        if (res == null)
        {
            return BadRequest("Asset exists.");
        }
        return Created();
    }


    [HttpPut("{id:Guid}")]
    public ActionResult<AssetResponseDto> Put(Guid id, AssetUpdateDto assetUpdate)
    {
        AssetResponseDto res = _assetServ.Edit(id, assetUpdate);
        if (res != null)
        {
            return Ok(res);
        }
        return NotFound($"Couldnot update Asset with id: {id}");
    }


    [HttpPatch("{id:Guid}/status")]
    public ActionResult Patch(Guid id)
    {
        bool res = _assetServ.ToggleStatus(id);
        if (res)
        {
            return Ok();
        }
        return NotFound($"Couldnot update status of Asset with id: {id}");
    }


    [HttpDelete("{id:Guid}")]
    public ActionResult<AssetResponseDto> Delete(Guid id)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"DELETE called for Id={id} at {DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff}");
        Console.WriteLine($"TraceIdentifier: {HttpContext.TraceIdentifier}");
        AssetResponseDto res = _assetServ.Delete(id);
        if (res != null)
        {
            return Ok(res);
        }
        return NotFound($"Couldnot Delete Asset with id: {id}");
    }
}

FILE: CustomerApi\Controllers\CustomerController.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Customer;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Polly.Timeout;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/customer")]


public class CustomerController : ControllerBase
{
    ICustomerService _custServ;


    public CustomerController(ICustomerService cs)
    {
        _custServ = cs;
    }




    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<CustomerFullDetailsDto>>> GetAsync()
    {
        return Ok(await _custServ.FetchAsync());
    }


    [HttpGet("me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerFullDetailsDto>> GetSelfAsync()
    {
        string? uId = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);
        Guid userId = Guid.Parse(uId);
        CustomerFullDetailsDto res = await _custServ.FetchByUserIdAsync(userId);
        if (res == null)
        {
            return NotFound("No Customer Found for the user");
        }
        return Ok(res);
    }


    [HttpGet("{custId:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerFullDetailsDto>> Get(Guid custId)
    {
        try
        {
            CustomerFullDetailsDto c = await _custServ.Fetch(custId);


            if (c != null)
            {
                return Ok(c);
            }
        }
        catch (TimeoutRejectedException ex)
        {
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status408RequestTimeout,
                new
                {
                    Status = 504,
                    Message = "InsuranceService did not respond within the allowed time."
                });
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            
            return BadRequest(ex.Message);
        }
        return NotFound($"Customer with id: {custId} Not Found");
    }


    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<ActionResult> Post(CustomerCreateDto custInp)
    {
        CustomerFullDetailsDto res =await _custServ.Create(custInp);
        if(res == null)
        {
            return BadRequest("Customer created for the UserId");
        }
        return Created();
    }


    //[HttpPut("{id:int}")]
    //public ActionResult<CustomerResponseDto> Put(int id, CustomerUpdateDto custUpdate)
    //{
    //    CustomerResponseDto res = _custServ.Edit(id, custUpdate);
    //    if (res!=null)
    //    {
    //        return Ok(res);
    //    }
    //    return NotFound($"Couldnot update Customer with id: {id}");
    //}


    [HttpPatch("{custId:Guid}/status")]
    public ActionResult Patch(Guid custId)
    {
        bool res = _custServ.ToggleStatus(custId);
        if (res)
        {
            return Ok();
        }
        return NotFound($"Couldnot update status of Customer with id: {custId}");
    }


    [HttpDelete("{custId:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerFullDetailsDto>> Delete(Guid custId)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"DELETE called for Id={custId} at {DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff}");
        Console.WriteLine($"TraceIdentifier: {HttpContext.TraceIdentifier}");
        CustomerFullDetailsDto res = await _custServ.Delete(custId);
        if (res != null)
        {
            return Ok(res);
        }
        return NotFound($"Couldnot Delete Customer with id: {custId}");
    }
}

FILE: CustomerApi\Middlewares\OcelotClaimsMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;


public sealed class OcelotClaimsMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> _logger;


    public OcelotClaimsMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        string? userId =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Id"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? username =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Name"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? email =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Email"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? userRole =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Role"].FirstOrDefault();


        _logger.LogInformation(
            "Identity headers received. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Email: {Email}, Role: {Role}",
            userId,
            username,
            email,
            userRole);


        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
        {
            var claims = new List<Claim>
            {
                new Claim(
                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier,
                    userId)
            };


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Name,
                        username));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Email,
                        email));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userRole))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Role,
                        userRole));
            }


            var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(
                claims,
                authenticationType: "OcelotGatewayAuth",
                nameType: ClaimTypes.Name,
                roleType: ClaimTypes.Role);


            context.User = new ClaimsPrincipal(identity);


            _logger.LogInformation(
                "ClaimsPrincipal created. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Role: {Role}",
                userId,
                username,
                userRole);
        }
        else
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "X-User-Id was not received from the API Gateway. No ClaimsPrincipal was created.");
        }


        await _next(context);
    }
}

FILE: CustomerApi\Program.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure;


var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


builder.Services.AddControllers();


builder.Services.AddAuthentication();
builder.Services.AddAuthorization();


builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();


builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer(); // 1. REQUIRED for routing discovery
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();


var app = builder.Build();


// 2. REQUIRED: Enable the middleware to serve generated Swagger JSON and UI
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
    {
        c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Customer API V1");
    });
}




app.UseMiddleware<OcelotClaimsMiddleware>();


app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();


app.MapControllers();


app.Run();

FILE: Earthquake.AgentApi.Application\Classes\Services\AgentService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Domain.Entities;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Classes.Services
{
    public class AgentService : IAgentService
    {
        private readonly IAgentRepository _repository;


        private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;


        public AgentService(IAgentRepository repository,HttpClient httpClient)
        {
            _repository = repository;
            _httpClient = httpClient;
        }


        public async Task<Guid>CreateCustomerAsync(Guid agentId,CreateCustomerRequestDto request)
        {
            var response =await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("customers",request);


            response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


            var customerId =
                await response.Content
                    .ReadFromJsonAsync<Guid>();


            await Audit(
                agentId,
                "Customer Created",
                customerId,
                null);


            return customerId;
        }


        public async Task<Guid>
            AddPropertyAsync(
            Guid agentId,
            AddPropertyRequestDto request)
        {
            var response =
                await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync(
                    "properties",
                    request);


            response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


            var propertyId =
                await response.Content
                    .ReadFromJsonAsync<Guid>();


            await Audit(
                agentId,
                "Property Added",
                request.CustomerId,
                propertyId);


            return propertyId;
        }


        public async Task<Guid>
            RaiseUnderwritingRequestAsync(
            Guid agentId,
            CreateUnderwritingRequestDto request)
        {
            var response =
                await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync(
                    "underwriting/requests",
                    request);


            response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


            var requestId =
                await response.Content
                    .ReadFromJsonAsync<Guid>();


            await Audit(
                agentId,
                "Underwriting Requested",
                null,
                request.PropertyId);


            return requestId;
        }


        public async Task<List<CustomerSummaryDto>>
            GetCustomersAsync()
        {
            return await _httpClient
                .GetFromJsonAsync<
                    List<CustomerSummaryDto>>(
                        "customers")
                   ?? [];
        }


        private async Task Audit(
            Guid agentId,
            string actionType,
            Guid? customerId,
            Guid? propertyId)
        {
            await _repository.AddActionAsync(
                new AgentAction
                {
                    ActionId = Guid.NewGuid(),
                    AgentId = agentId,
                    ActionType = actionType,
                    CustomerId = customerId,
                    PropertyId = propertyId,
                    PerformedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
                });


            await _repository.SaveChangesAsync();
        }
    }   
}

using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;


namespace Earthquake.AgentApi.Application;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IAgentService, AgentService>();


        services.AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(client =>
        {
            client.BaseAddress =
                new Uri("https://localhost:5050/gateway/");
        });


        return services;
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.AgentApi.Domain.Entities;
public class AgentAction
{
    [Key]
    [DatabaseGenerated(DatabaseGeneratedOption.Identity)]
    public Guid ActionId { get; set; }


    public Guid AgentId { get; set; }


    public string ActionType { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    public Guid? CustomerId { get; set; }


    public Guid? PropertyId { get; set; }


    public DateTime PerformedAt { get; set; }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure\Classes\Repository\AgentRepository.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.Classes.Repository
{
    public class AgentRepository : IAgentRepository
    {
        private readonly AgentDbContext _context;


        public AgentRepository(
            AgentDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }


        public async Task AddActionAsync(
            AgentAction action)
        {
            await _context.AgentActions
                .AddAsync(action);
        }


        public async Task<List<AgentAction>>
            GetActionsAsync(Guid agentId)
        {
            return await _context.AgentActions
                .Where(x => x.AgentId == agentId)
                .ToListAsync();
        }


        public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
        {
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
        }
    }
}




================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure\DependencyInjection.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.Classes.Repository;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Http;




public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(
        this IServiceCollection services,
        IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.AddDbContext<AgentDbContext>(options =>
            options.UseSqlServer(
                configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnectionUrl")));


        services.AddScoped<IAgentRepository,
                           AgentRepository>();




        services.AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(client =>
        {
            client.BaseAddress =
                new Uri("https://localhost:5050/gateway/");
        });


        return services;
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AgentApi\Controllers\AgentController.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


namespace Earthquake.AgentApi.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/agents")]
    //[Authorize(Roles = "Agent")]
    public class AgentController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly IAgentService _service;


        public AgentController(
            IAgentService service)
        {
            _service = service;
        }


        [HttpGet("customers")]
        public async Task<IActionResult>
            GetCustomers()
        {
            return Ok(
                await _service.GetCustomersAsync());
        }


        [HttpPost("customers")]
        public async Task<IActionResult>
            CreateCustomer(
                CreateCustomerRequestDto request)
        {
            var agentId =
                Guid.Parse(
                    Request.Headers["X-User-Id"]!);


            var result =
                await _service
                    .CreateCustomerAsync(
                        agentId,
                        request);


            return Ok(result);
        }


        [HttpPost("properties")]
        public async Task<IActionResult>
            AddProperty(
                AddPropertyRequestDto request)
        {
            var agentId =
                Guid.Parse(
                    Request.Headers["X-User-Id"]!);


            var result =
                await _service
                    .AddPropertyAsync(
                        agentId,
                        request);


            return Ok(result);
        }


        [HttpPost("underwriting-requests")]
        public async Task<IActionResult>
            RaiseRequest(
                CreateUnderwritingRequestDto request)
        {
            var agentId =
                Guid.Parse(
                    Request.Headers["X-User-Id"]!);


            var result =
                await _service
                    .RaiseUnderwritingRequestAsync(
                        agentId,
                        request);


            return Ok(result);
        }
    }
}

using Earthquake.AgentApi.Application;


var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


builder.Services.AddControllers();


builder.Services.AddAuthentication();
builder.Services.AddAuthorization();


builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);


builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer(); // 1. REQUIRED for routing discovery
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();


var app = builder.Build();


// 2. REQUIRED: Enable the middleware to serve generated Swagger JSON and UI
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
    {
        c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Policy API V1");
    });
}


app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();


app.MapControllers();


app.Run();

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Application\Classes\Services\AuthService.cs
================================================================================
using Auth.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Security;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using BCrypt.Net;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Classes.Services;
public class AuthService : IAuthService
{
    private readonly IUserRepository _userRepository;


    private readonly IRoleRepository _roleRepository;


    private readonly IRefreshTokenRepository
        _refreshRepository;


    private readonly IJwtService _jwtService;


    public AuthService(
        IUserRepository userRepository,
        IRoleRepository roleRepository,
        IRefreshTokenRepository refreshRepository,
        IJwtService jwtService)
    {
        _userRepository = userRepository;


        _roleRepository = roleRepository;


        _refreshRepository = refreshRepository;


        _jwtService = jwtService;
    }


    public async Task<Guid> RegisterAsync(
        RegisterRequestDto request)
    {
        var existingUser =
            await _userRepository
                .GetByEmailAsync(request.Email);


        if (existingUser != null)
            throw new Exception("User exists");


        var role =
            await _roleRepository
                .GetByNameAsync(request.Role);


        if (role == null)
            throw new Exception("Invalid role");


        var user = new User
        {
            UserId = Guid.NewGuid(),


            FirstName = request.FirstName,


            LastName = request.LastName,


            Email = request.Email,


            PhoneNumber = request.PhoneNumber,


            PasswordHash =
                BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(
                    request.Password),


            RoleId = role.RoleId
        };


        await _userRepository.AddAsync(user);


        await _userRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return user.UserId;
    }


    public async Task<AuthResponseDto> LoginAsync(
        LoginRequestDto request)
    {
        var user =
            await _userRepository
                .GetByEmailAsync(request.Email);


        if (user == null)
            throw new Exception("Invalid Login");


        bool validPassword =
            BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(
                request.Password,
                user.PasswordHash);


        if (!validPassword)
            throw new Exception("Invalid Login");


        var accessToken =
            _jwtService.GenerateToken(user);


        var refreshToken =
            _jwtService.GenerateRefreshToken();


        await _refreshRepository.AddAsync(
            new RefreshToken
            {
                RefreshTokenId = Guid.NewGuid(),


                UserId = user.UserId,


                Token = refreshToken,


                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,


                ExpiresAt = DateTime.UtcNow
                    .AddDays(7)
            });


        await _refreshRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return new AuthResponseDto
        {
            AccessToken = accessToken,


            RefreshToken = refreshToken,


            UserId = user.UserId,


            Email = user.Email,


            FullName =
                $"{user.FirstName} {user.LastName}",


            Role = user.Role.Name,


            ExpiresAt =
                DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(1)
        };
    }


    //public async Task<AuthResponseDto>
    //    RefreshTokenAsync(
    //    string refreshToken)
    //{
    //    throw new NotImplementedException();
    //}


    //public async Task LogoutAsync(
    //    string refreshToken)
    //{
    //    throw new NotImplementedException();
    //}


    public async Task<UserDto?>
        GetCurrentUserAsync(Guid userId)
    {
        var user =
            await _userRepository
                .GetByIdAsync(userId);


        if (user == null)
            return null;


        return new UserDto
        {
            UserId = user.UserId,


            Email = user.Email,


            FullName =
                $"{user.FirstName} {user.LastName}",


            Role = user.Role.Name
        };
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Application\Classes\Services\UserService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Classes.Services
{
    public class UserService : IUserService
    {
        private readonly IUserRepository _userRepo;
        public UserService(IUserRepository userRepo) {
            _userRepo = userRepo;
        }


        public async Task<User> GetUserById(Guid id)
        {
            User us = await _userRepo.GetByIdAsync(id);
            return us;
        }


        public async Task<List<User>> GetUserBatch(List<Guid> request)
        {
            List<User> us = await _userRepo.GetUsersBatch(request);
            return us;
        }


        public async Task<List<User>> GetAllUsers()
        {
            return await _userRepo.GetAllAsync();
        }
    }
}

using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Security;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;




namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Application;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddValidatorsFromAssembly(typeof(DependencyInjection).Assembly);


        services.AddScoped<IAuthService, AuthService>();
        services.AddScoped<IUserService, UserService>();


        return services;
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure\DependencyInjection.cs
================================================================================
using Auth.Infrastructure.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Configuration;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(
        this IServiceCollection services, 
        IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        // Configure strongly typed settings
        services.Configure<JwtSettings>(configuration.GetSection(JwtSettings.SectionName));


        // Configure EF Core DbContext with SQL Server
        var connectionString = configuration.GetConnectionString("AuthDatabase")
            ?? configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")
            ?? "Server=(localdb)\\mssqllocaldb;Database=EarthquakeAuthDb;Trusted_Connection=True;MultipleActiveResultSets=true;TrustServerCertificate=True";


        services.AddDbContext<AuthDbContext>(options =>
        {
            options.UseSqlServer(connectionString, sqlOptions =>
            {
                sqlOptions.MigrationsAssembly(typeof(AuthDbContext).Assembly.FullName);
                sqlOptions.EnableRetryOnFailure(
                    maxRetryCount: 5,
                    maxRetryDelay: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),
                    errorNumbersToAdd: null);
            });
        });
        services.AddScoped<IRoleRepository, RoleRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IRefreshTokenRepository, RefreshTokenRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IUserRepository, UserRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IJwtService, JwtService>();






        return services;
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure\JwtService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Configuration;


namespace Auth.Infrastructure.Services;


public class JwtService : IJwtService
{
    private readonly JwtSettings _jwtSettings;


    public JwtService(
        IOptions<JwtSettings> jwtSettings)
    {
        _jwtSettings = jwtSettings.Value;
    }


    public string GenerateToken(User user)
    {
        var key = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_jwtSettings.SecretKey));


        var credentials =new SigningCredentials(key,SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);


        var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier,user.UserId.ToString()),


            new(ClaimTypes.Email,user.Email),


            new(ClaimTypes.Name,$"{user.FirstName} {user.LastName}"),


            new(ClaimTypes.Role,user.Role.Name)
        };


        var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
                issuer: _jwtSettings.Issuer,


                audience: _jwtSettings.Audience,


                claims: claims,


                expires: DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(_jwtSettings.AccessTokenExpirationMinutes),


                signingCredentials: credentials
        );


        return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
    }


    public string GenerateRefreshToken()
    {
        var randomBytes = RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(64);


        return Convert.ToBase64String(
            randomBytes);
    }


    public Guid? GetUserIdFromExpiredToken(
        string accessToken)
    {
        var tokenValidationParameters =
            new TokenValidationParameters
            {
                ValidateAudience = true,


                ValidateIssuer = true,


                ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,


                ValidateLifetime = false,


                ValidIssuer = _jwtSettings.Issuer,


                ValidAudience = _jwtSettings.Audience,


                IssuerSigningKey =
                    new SymmetricSecurityKey(
                        Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(
                            _jwtSettings.SecretKey))
            };


        var tokenHandler =
            new JwtSecurityTokenHandler();


        try
        {
            var principal =
                tokenHandler.ValidateToken(
                    accessToken,
                    tokenValidationParameters,
                    out SecurityToken securityToken);


            var userIdClaim =
                principal.FindFirst(
                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);


            if (userIdClaim == null)
                return null;


            return Guid.Parse(
                userIdClaim.Value);
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure\Persistence\AuthDbContext.cs

using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
public class AuthDbContext : DbContext
{
    public AuthDbContext(
        DbContextOptions<AuthDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }


    public DbSet<User> Users => Set<User>();


    public DbSet<Role> Roles => Set<Role>();


    public DbSet<RefreshToken> RefreshTokens
        => Set<RefreshToken>();


    protected override void OnModelCreating(
        ModelBuilder builder)
    {
        base.OnModelCreating(builder);


        builder.Entity<Role>()
            .HasIndex(x => x.Name)
            .IsUnique();


        builder.Entity<User>()
            .HasIndex(x => x.Email)
            .IsUnique();
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure\Repositories\RefreshTokenRepository.cs

using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;
public class RefreshTokenRepository
    : IRefreshTokenRepository
{
    private readonly AuthDbContext _context;


    public RefreshTokenRepository(
        AuthDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }


    public async Task<RefreshToken?> GetByTokenAsync(
        string token)
    {
        return await _context.RefreshTokens
            .Include(x => x.User)
            .ThenInclude(x => x.Role)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(
                x => x.Token == token);
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(
        RefreshToken token)
    {
        await _context.RefreshTokens
            .AddAsync(token);
    }


    public void Update(
        RefreshToken token)
    {
        _context.RefreshTokens
            .Update(token);
    }


    public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
    {
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;
public class UserRepository : IUserRepository
{
    private readonly AuthDbContext _context;


    public UserRepository(AuthDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }


    public async Task<User?> GetByEmailAsync(string email)
    {
        return await _context.Users.Include(x => x.Role).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Email == email);
    }


    public async Task<User?> GetByIdAsync(Guid userId)
    {
        return await _context.Users.Include(x => x.Role).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.UserId == userId);
    }


    public async Task<List<User>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await _context.Users.Include(x => x.Role).ToListAsync();
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(User user)
    {
        await _context.Users.AddAsync(user);
    }


    public void Update(User user)
    {
        _context.Users.Update(user);
    }


    public void Delete(User user)
    {
        _context.Users.Remove(user);
    }


    public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
    {
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task<List<User>> GetUsersBatch(List<Guid> requestids)
    {
        return await _context.Users
       .Include(u => u.Role) // Include the role if you need to pass it back to the customer service
       .Where(u => requestids.Contains(u.UserId))
       .ToListAsync();
    }
}

using Auth.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Security;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.Controllers;
[ApiController]
[Route("api/user")]


public class UserController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IUserService _userService;
    public UserController(IUserService _userServ)
    {
        _userService = _userServ;
    }


    [HttpGet("")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<User>>> GetUsersAsync()
    { 
        
        return Ok(await _userService.GetAllUsers());
    }




    [HttpGet("{id:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<User>> GetUserAsync(Guid id)
    {
        User us = await _userService.GetUserById(id);
        if (us == null)
        {
            return NotFound("User Doesnot Exist");
        }
        return Ok(us);
    }




    [HttpPost("batch")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<User>>> GetUserBatchAsync(List<Guid> request)
    {
        foreach (Guid item in request)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(item);
        }
        List<User> us = await _userService.GetUserBatch(request);


        if (us == null)
        {
            return NotFound("Users Doesnot Exist");
        }
        Console.WriteLine("Returning " + us.Count);
        return Ok(us);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi\Middleware\CorrelationIdMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using Serilog.Context;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.API.Middleware;


public class CorrelationIdMiddleware
{
    private const string CorrelationIdHeaderName = "X-Correlation-ID";
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;


    public CorrelationIdMiddleware(RequestDelegate _next)
    {
        this._next = _next;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        string correlationId;


        if (context.Request.Headers.TryGetValue(CorrelationIdHeaderName, out var incomingCorrelationId) &&
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(incomingCorrelationId))
        {
            correlationId = incomingCorrelationId.ToString();
        }
        else
        {
            correlationId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("D");
        }


        context.TraceIdentifier = correlationId;
        context.Response.Headers[CorrelationIdHeaderName] = correlationId;


        using (LogContext.PushProperty("CorrelationId", correlationId))
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
    }
}




================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi\Middleware\GlobalExceptionMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.Net;
using System.Security.Authentication;
using System.Text.Json;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application.DTOs;


namespace Earthquake.AuthApi.API.Middleware;


public class GlobalExceptionMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<GlobalExceptionMiddleware> _logger;
    private readonly IHostEnvironment _environment;


    public GlobalExceptionMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<GlobalExceptionMiddleware> logger,
        IHostEnvironment environment)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
        _environment = environment;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            await HandleExceptionAsync(context, ex);
        }
    }


    private async Task HandleExceptionAsync(HttpContext context, Exception exception)
    {
        var traceId = context.TraceIdentifier;
        var statusCode = HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError;
        var message = "An unexpected error occurred. Please contact system support.";
        IDictionary<string, string[]>? errors = null;


        switch (exception)
        {
            case ValidationException validationException:
                statusCode = HttpStatusCode.BadRequest;
                message = validationException.Message;
                _logger.LogWarning("Validation failure for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
                break;


            case AuthenticationException authException:
                statusCode = HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized;
                message = authException.Message;
                _logger.LogWarning("Authentication failed for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
                break;


            //case AuthorizationException authzException:
            //    statusCode = HttpStatusCode.Forbidden;
            //    message = authzException.Message;
            //    _logger.LogWarning("Authorization failed for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
            //    break;


            //case NotFoundException notFoundException:
            //    statusCode = HttpStatusCode.NotFound;
            //    message = notFoundException.Message;
            //    _logger.LogInformation("Resource not found for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
            //    break;


            //case ConflictException conflictException:
            //    statusCode = HttpStatusCode.Conflict;
            //    message = conflictException.Message;
            //    _logger.LogWarning("Conflict detected for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
            //    break;


            //case TokenException tokenException:
            //    statusCode = HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized;
            //    message = tokenException.Message;
            //    _logger.LogWarning("Token security error for trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, message);
            //    break;


            default:
                _logger.LogError(exception, "Unhandled exception occurred during request with trace {TraceId}: {Message}", traceId, exception.Message);
                if (_environment.IsDevelopment())
                {
                    message = exception.Message;
                }
                break;
        }


        context.Response.ContentType = "application/json";
        context.Response.StatusCode = (int)statusCode;


        var responseDto = new ErrorResponseDto((int)statusCode, message, traceId, errors);
        var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(responseDto, new JsonSerializerOptions
        {
            PropertyNamingPolicy = JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase
        });


        await context.Response.WriteAsync(json);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.AuthApi\Program.cs
================================================================================
using Serilog;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.API.Middleware;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Application;
using Earthquake.AuthApi.Infrastructure;


// Initialize early bootstrap Serilog logger
Log.Logger = new LoggerConfiguration()
    .WriteTo.Console()
    .CreateBootstrapLogger();


try
{
    Log.Information("Starting Earthquake AuthGateway API...");


    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


    // Configure Serilog from configuration
    builder.Host.UseSerilog((context, services, configuration) => configuration
        .ReadFrom.Configuration(context.Configuration)
        .ReadFrom.Services(services)
        .Enrich.FromLogContext()
        .WriteTo.Console(outputTemplate: "[{Timestamp:HH:mm:ss} {Level:u3}] [{CorrelationId}] {Message:lj}{NewLine}{Exception}"));


    // Register Clean Architecture layers
    builder.Services.AddApplication();
    builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);


    builder.Services.AddControllers();


    // Swagger Documentation Support Services
    builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
    builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
    {
        options.SwaggerDoc("v1", new Microsoft.OpenApi.Models.OpenApiInfo
        {
            Title = "Earthquake AuthGateway",
            Version = "v1"
        });
    });


    var app = builder.Build();


    // Pipeline: Exception handling -> Correlation ID -> Structured Logging -> HTTPS -> CORS -> AuthN -> AuthZ -> Endpoints
    app.UseMiddleware<GlobalExceptionMiddleware>();
    app.UseMiddleware<CorrelationIdMiddleware>();


    app.UseSerilogRequestLogging();


    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.UseSwagger();
        app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
        {
            c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Earthquake AuthGateway v1");
            c.RoutePrefix = string.Empty; // Serve Swagger UI at root "/"
        });
    }


    app.UseHttpsRedirection();


    app.UseAuthentication();
    app.UseAuthorization();


    app.MapControllers();


    // Controlled automatic role seeding and development admin setup
    //try
    //{
    //    await DatabaseSeeder.SeedAsync(app.Services);
    //}
    //catch (Exception ex)
    //{
    //    Log.Warning(ex, "Initial database seeding skipped or deferred: {Message}", ex.Message);
    //}


    Log.Information("Earthquake AuthGateway started successfully.");
    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception ex) when (ex is not HostAbortedException)
{
    Log.Fatal(ex, "Earthquake AuthGateway terminated unexpectedly.");
}
finally
{
    Log.CloseAndFlush();
}
