================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application\Classes\Services\ClaimService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Inteafaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class ClaimService : IClaimService
{
    private const int ActivePolicyStatus = 2;
    private const int MaximumProofsPerClaim = 10;


    private readonly IClaimRepository _claimRepository;
    private readonly IClaimProofRepository _claimProofRepository;
    private readonly IClaimFileService _claimFileService;
    private readonly IGatewayClient _gatewayClient;


    public ClaimService(
        IClaimRepository claimRepository,
        IClaimProofRepository claimProofRepository,
        IClaimFileService claimFileService,
        IGatewayClient gatewayClient)
    {
        _claimRepository = claimRepository;
        _claimProofRepository = claimProofRepository;
        _claimFileService = claimFileService;
        _gatewayClient = gatewayClient;
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> CreateAsync(
        ClaimCreateDto dto,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        PolicySummaryDto? policy =
            await _gatewayClient.GetPolicyAsync(
                dto.PolicyId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                "The selected policy was not found.");
        }


        if (policy.Status != ActivePolicyStatus)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "A claim can only be created for an active policy.");
        }


        if (dto.IncidentDate.Date > DateTime.UtcNow.Date)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The incident date cannot be in the future.");
        }


        if (dto.IncidentDate.Date < policy.EffectiveDate.Date ||
            dto.IncidentDate.Date > policy.ExpiryDate.Date)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The incident date must be within the policy coverage period.");
        }


        if (policy.PropertyId != Guid.Empty &&
            dto.AssetId != policy.PropertyId)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The selected property does not belong to the selected policy.");
        }


        if (policy.SumInsured > 0 &&
            dto.ClaimedAmount > policy.SumInsured)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"The claimed amount cannot exceed the policy sum insured of {policy.SumInsured:N2}.");
        }


        Guid customerId;


        if (IsCustomer(authenticatedRole))
        {
            CurrentCustomerDto? customer =
                await _gatewayClient.GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
                    cancellationToken);


            if (customer is null)
            {
                throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                    "A customer profile was not found for the authenticated user.");
            }


            if (!customer.IsActive)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "The customer profile is inactive.");
            }


            customerId = customer.CustomerId;


            if (policy.CustomerId != Guid.Empty &&
                policy.CustomerId != customerId)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                    "The selected policy does not belong to the authenticated customer.");
            }
        }
        else if (IsAgent(authenticatedRole))
        {
            if (!dto.CustomerId.HasValue ||
                dto.CustomerId.Value == Guid.Empty)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "CustomerId is required when an Agent creates a claim.");
            }


            customerId = dto.CustomerId.Value;


            if (policy.CustomerId != Guid.Empty &&
                policy.CustomerId != customerId)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "The selected policy does not belong to the selected customer.");
            }
        }
        else
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "Only a Customer or Agent can create a claim.");
        }


        DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;


        var claim = new Claim
        {
            ClaimId = Guid.NewGuid(),
            ClaimNumber = await GenerateClaimNumberAsync(
                cancellationToken),
            PolicyId = policy.PolicyId,
            AssetId = policy.PropertyId,
            CustomerId = customerId,
            ReportedByUserId = authenticatedUserId,
            ReportedByRole = authenticatedRole,
            IncidentDate = dto.IncidentDate,
            IncidentType = dto.IncidentType.Trim(),
            IncidentLocation = dto.IncidentLocation.Trim(),
            Description = dto.Description.Trim(),
            ClaimedAmount = dto.ClaimedAmount,
            Status = ClaimStatus.Draft,
            CreatedAt = now,
            UpdatedAt = now
        };


        await _claimRepository.AddAsync(
            claim,
            cancellationToken);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimResponseDto>> GetMyClaimsAsync(
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        List<Claim> claims;


        if (IsCustomer(authenticatedRole))
        {
            CurrentCustomerDto? customer =
                await _gatewayClient.GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
                    cancellationToken);


            if (customer is null)
            {
                return new List<ClaimResponseDto>();
            }


            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetByCustomerIdAsync(
                    customer.CustomerId,
                    cancellationToken);
        }
        else if (IsAgent(authenticatedRole))
        {
            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetByReportedByUserIdAsync(
                    authenticatedUserId,
                    cancellationToken);
        }
        else if (IsClaimManager(authenticatedRole))
        {
            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetByManagerIdAsync(
                    authenticatedUserId,
                    cancellationToken);
        }
        else if (IsAdmin(authenticatedRole))
        {
            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetAllAsync(
                    cancellationToken);
        }
        else
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The authenticated role cannot view claims.");
        }


        return claims
            .Select(MapClaim)
            .ToList();
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimResponseDto>> GetAllAsync(
        ClaimStatus? status = null,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        List<Claim> claims;


        if (status.HasValue)
        {
            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetByStatusAsync(
                    status.Value,
                    cancellationToken);
        }
        else
        {
            claims =
                await _claimRepository.GetAllAsync(
                    cancellationToken);
        }


        return claims
            .Select(MapClaim)
            .ToList();
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimResponseDto>> GetAssignedToMeAsync(
        Guid claimManagerId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        List<Claim> claims =
            await _claimRepository.GetByManagerIdAsync(
                claimManagerId,
                cancellationToken);


        return claims
            .Select(MapClaim)
            .ToList();
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto?> GetByIdAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim? claim =
            await _claimRepository.GetByIdWithProofsAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (claim is null)
        {
            return null;
        }


        await EnsureCanViewClaimAsync(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole,
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }
    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> UpdateAsync(
    Guid claimId,
    ClaimUpdateDto dto,
    Guid authenticatedUserId,
    string authenticatedRole,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        EnsureCreator(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.Draft)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only a draft claim can be updated.");
        }


        if (dto.IncidentDate.Date > DateTime.UtcNow.Date)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The incident date cannot be in the future.");
        }


        PolicySummaryDto? policy =
            await _gatewayClient.GetPolicyAsync(
                claim.PolicyId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                "The policy associated with this claim was not found.");
        }


        if (policy.Status != ActivePolicyStatus)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The policy associated with this claim is no longer active.");
        }


        if (dto.IncidentDate.Date < policy.EffectiveDate.Date ||
            dto.IncidentDate.Date > policy.ExpiryDate.Date)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The incident date must be within the policy coverage period.");
        }


        if (policy.SumInsured > 0 &&
            dto.ClaimedAmount > policy.SumInsured)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"The claimed amount cannot exceed the policy sum insured of {policy.SumInsured:N2}.");
        }


        claim.IncidentDate = dto.IncidentDate;
        claim.IncidentType = dto.IncidentType.Trim();
        claim.IncidentLocation = dto.IncidentLocation.Trim();
        claim.Description = dto.Description.Trim();
        claim.ClaimedAmount = dto.ClaimedAmount;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> SubmitAsync(
    Guid claimId,
    Guid authenticatedUserId,
    string authenticatedRole,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        EnsureCreator(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.Draft)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only a draft claim can be submitted.");
        }


        int proofCount =
            await _claimProofRepository.CountByClaimIdAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (proofCount == 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Upload at least one image or PDF proof before submitting the claim.");
        }


        PolicySummaryDto? policy =
            await _gatewayClient.GetPolicyAsync(
                claim.PolicyId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                "The policy associated with this claim was not found.");
        }


        if (policy.Status != ActivePolicyStatus)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The claim cannot be submitted because the policy is not active.");
        }


        if (claim.IncidentDate.Date < policy.EffectiveDate.Date ||
            claim.IncidentDate.Date > policy.ExpiryDate.Date)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The incident date is outside the policy coverage period.");
        }


        if (policy.PropertyId != Guid.Empty &&
            claim.AssetId != policy.PropertyId)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The property associated with the claim does not match the policy.");
        }


        if (policy.CustomerId != Guid.Empty &&
            claim.CustomerId != policy.CustomerId)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The customer associated with the claim does not match the policy.");
        }


        if (policy.SumInsured > 0 &&
            claim.ClaimedAmount > policy.SumInsured)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"The claimed amount cannot exceed the policy sum insured of {policy.SumInsured:N2}.");
        }


        claim.Status = ClaimStatus.Submitted;
        claim.SubmittedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }
    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> ReviewAsync(
    Guid claimId,
    ClaimReviewDto dto,
    Guid claimManagerId,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.UnderReview)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The claim must be under review before a decision can be recorded.");
        }


        if (claim.ClaimManagerId != claimManagerId)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "Only the assigned Claim Manager can review this claim.");
        }


        PolicySummaryDto? policy =
            await _gatewayClient.GetPolicyAsync(
                claim.PolicyId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                "The policy associated with this claim was not found.");
        }


        ValidateReviewAmounts(
            claim,
            policy,
            dto);


        claim.AssessedAmount = dto.AssessedAmount;
        claim.ManagerRemarks = dto.ManagerRemarks.Trim();
        claim.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        if (dto.Decision == ClaimDecision.Rejected)
        {
            claim.Status = ClaimStatus.Rejected;
            claim.ApprovedAmount = 0;
            claim.DeductibleAmount = 0;
            claim.FinalSettlementAmount = 0;
        }
        else
        {
            decimal deductibleAmount =
                Math.Round(
                    dto.ApprovedAmount *
                    policy.DeductiblePercentage /
                    100m,
                    2);


            decimal finalSettlementAmount =
                Math.Max(
                    dto.ApprovedAmount -
                    deductibleAmount,
                    0);


            claim.ApprovedAmount =
                dto.ApprovedAmount;


            claim.DeductibleAmount =
                deductibleAmount;


            claim.FinalSettlementAmount =
                finalSettlementAmount;


            claim.Status =
                dto.Decision == ClaimDecision.Approved
                    ? ClaimStatus.Approved
                    : ClaimStatus.PartiallyApproved;
        }


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> SettleAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        ClaimSettlementDto dto,
        Guid claimManagerId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        bool canBeSettled =
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.Approved ||
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.PartiallyApproved;


        if (!canBeSettled)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only an approved or partially approved claim can be settled.");
        }


        if (claim.ClaimManagerId != claimManagerId)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "Only the assigned Claim Manager can settle this claim.");
        }


        if (!claim.FinalSettlementAmount.HasValue ||
            claim.FinalSettlementAmount.Value <= 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The claim does not have a valid settlement amount.");
        }


        claim.SettlementReference =
            dto.SettlementReference.Trim();


        claim.Status = ClaimStatus.Settled;
        claim.SettledAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimProofResponseDto>> UploadProofsAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        IReadOnlyCollection<IFormFile> files,
        string? description,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        EnsureCreator(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.Draft)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Proofs can only be uploaded while the claim is in Draft status.");
        }


        if (files.Count == 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Select at least one image or PDF file.");
        }


        int currentProofCount =
            await _claimProofRepository.CountByClaimIdAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (currentProofCount + files.Count >
            MaximumProofsPerClaim)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"A claim can contain a maximum of {MaximumProofsPerClaim} proof files.");
        }


        var uploadedProofs =
            new List<ClaimProofResponseDto>();


        foreach (IFormFile file in files)
        {
            SavedClaimFile savedFile =
                await _claimFileService.SaveFileAsync(
                    claimId,
                    file,
                    cancellationToken);


            var proof = new ClaimProof
            {
                ClaimProofId = Guid.NewGuid(),
                ClaimId = claimId,
                OriginalFileName =
                    Path.GetFileName(file.FileName),
                StoredFileName =
                    savedFile.StoredFileName,
                FilePath =
                    savedFile.RelativeFilePath,
                ContentType =
                    savedFile.ContentType,
                FileSize =
                    savedFile.FileSize,
                Description =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(description)
                        ? null
                        : description.Trim(),
                UploadedByUserId =
                    authenticatedUserId,
                UploadedAt =
                    DateTime.UtcNow
            };


            try
            {
                await _claimProofRepository.AddAsync(
                    proof,
                    cancellationToken);


                await _claimProofRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
                    cancellationToken);
            }
            catch
            {
                await _claimFileService.DeleteFileAsync(
                    savedFile.RelativeFilePath,
                    cancellationToken);


                throw;
            }


            uploadedProofs.Add(
                MapProof(proof));
        }


        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return uploadedProofs;
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimProofResponseDto>> GetProofsAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        await EnsureCanViewClaimAsync(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole,
            cancellationToken);


        List<ClaimProof> proofs =
            await _claimProofRepository.GetByClaimIdAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        return proofs
            .Select(MapProof)
            .ToList();
    }


    public async Task DeleteProofAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid proofId,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim =
            await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        EnsureCreator(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.Draft)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Proofs can only be deleted while the claim is in Draft status.");
        }


        ClaimProof? proof =
            await _claimProofRepository.GetByIdAsync(
                proofId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (proof is null ||
            proof.ClaimId != claimId)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                "The selected proof file was not found.");
        }


        _claimProofRepository.Delete(proof);


        await _claimProofRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        await _claimFileService.DeleteFileAsync(
            proof.FilePath,
            cancellationToken);


        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);
    }


    private async Task<Claim> GetRequiredClaimAsync(
    Guid claimId,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        Claim? claim =
            await _claimRepository.GetByIdAsync(
                claimId,
                cancellationToken);


        if (claim is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                $"Claim with ID '{claimId}' was not found.");
        }


        return claim;
    }


    private async Task EnsureCanViewClaimAsync(
        Claim claim,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (IsAdmin(authenticatedRole))
        {
            return;
        }


        if (IsClaimManager(authenticatedRole))
        {
            if (claim.Status == ClaimStatus.Draft)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                    "Claim Managers cannot view customer draft claims.");
            }


            return;
        }


        if (IsAgent(authenticatedRole))
        {
            if (claim.ReportedByUserId != authenticatedUserId)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                    "You are not permitted to view this claim.");
            }


            return;
        }


        if (IsCustomer(authenticatedRole))
        {
            CurrentCustomerDto? customer =
                await _gatewayClient.GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
                    cancellationToken);


            if (customer is null ||
                customer.CustomerId != claim.CustomerId)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                    "You are not permitted to view this claim.");
            }


            return;
        }


        throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
            "You are not permitted to view this claim.");
    }


    private static void EnsureCreator(
        Claim claim,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole)
    {
        if (!IsCustomer(authenticatedRole) &&
            !IsAgent(authenticatedRole))
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "Only a Customer or Agent can perform this operation.");
        }


        if (claim.ReportedByUserId != authenticatedUserId)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "Only the user who created this claim can perform this operation.");
        }
    }


    private static void ValidateReviewAmounts(
        Claim claim,
        PolicySummaryDto policy,
        ClaimReviewDto dto)
    {
        if (dto.Decision == ClaimDecision.Rejected)
        {
            if (dto.ApprovedAmount != 0)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Approved amount must be zero for a rejected claim.");
            }


            return;
        }


        if (dto.AssessedAmount <= 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Assessed amount must be greater than zero.");
        }


        if (dto.ApprovedAmount <= 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Approved amount must be greater than zero.");
        }


        if (dto.ApprovedAmount >
            dto.AssessedAmount)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Approved amount cannot exceed the assessed amount.");
        }


        if (dto.ApprovedAmount >
            claim.ClaimedAmount)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Approved amount cannot exceed the claimed amount.");
        }


        if (policy.SumInsured > 0 &&
            dto.ApprovedAmount > policy.SumInsured)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Approved amount cannot exceed the policy sum insured.");
        }


        if (dto.Decision ==
                ClaimDecision.PartiallyApproved &&
            dto.ApprovedAmount >= claim.ClaimedAmount)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "A partially approved amount must be lower than the claimed amount.");
        }


        if (dto.Decision ==
                ClaimDecision.Approved &&
            dto.ApprovedAmount != claim.ClaimedAmount)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "For full approval, the approved amount must equal the claimed amount.");
        }
    }


    private async Task<string> GenerateClaimNumberAsync(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++)
        {
            string claimNumber =
                $"CLM-{DateTime.UtcNow:yyyyMMdd}-" +
                $"{Random.Shared.Next(100000, 999999)}";


            Claim? existing =
                await _claimRepository.GetByClaimNumberAsync(
                    claimNumber,
                    cancellationToken);


            if (existing is null)
            {
                return claimNumber;
            }
        }


        string uniquePart =
            Guid.NewGuid()
                .ToString("N")[..8]
                .ToUpperInvariant();


        return
            $"CLM-{DateTime.UtcNow:yyyyMMdd}-{uniquePart}";
    }


    private static ClaimResponseDto MapClaim(
        Claim claim)
    {
        return new ClaimResponseDto
        {
            ClaimId = claim.ClaimId,
            ClaimNumber = claim.ClaimNumber,
            PolicyId = claim.PolicyId,
            AssetId = claim.AssetId,
            CustomerId = claim.CustomerId,
            ReportedByUserId = claim.ReportedByUserId,
            ReportedByRole = claim.ReportedByRole,
            ClaimManagerId = claim.ClaimManagerId,
            IncidentDate = claim.IncidentDate,
            IncidentType = claim.IncidentType,
            IncidentLocation = claim.IncidentLocation,
            Description = claim.Description,
            ClaimedAmount = claim.ClaimedAmount,
            AssessedAmount = claim.AssessedAmount,
            ApprovedAmount = claim.ApprovedAmount,
            DeductibleAmount = claim.DeductibleAmount,
            FinalSettlementAmount =
                claim.FinalSettlementAmount,
            Status = claim.Status,
            StatusName = claim.Status.ToString(),
            ManagerRemarks = claim.ManagerRemarks,
            SettlementReference =
                claim.SettlementReference,
            CreatedAt = claim.CreatedAt,
            SubmittedAt = claim.SubmittedAt,
            ReviewedAt = claim.ReviewedAt,
            DecidedAt = claim.DecidedAt,
            SettledAt = claim.SettledAt,
            UpdatedAt = claim.UpdatedAt,
            Proofs = claim.Proofs
                .Select(MapProof)
                .ToList()
        };
    }


    private static ClaimProofResponseDto MapProof(
        ClaimProof proof)
    {
        string normalizedPath =
            proof.FilePath
                .Replace("\\", "/")
                .TrimStart('/');


        return new ClaimProofResponseDto
        {
            ClaimProofId = proof.ClaimProofId,
            ClaimId = proof.ClaimId,
            OriginalFileName =
                proof.OriginalFileName,
            ContentType = proof.ContentType,
            FileSize = proof.FileSize,
            Description = proof.Description,
            FileUrl = $"/{normalizedPath}",
            UploadedByUserId =
                proof.UploadedByUserId,
            UploadedAt = proof.UploadedAt
        };
    }


    private static bool IsCustomer(
        string role)
    {
        return string.Equals(
            role,
            "Customer",
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }


    private static bool IsAgent(
        string role)
    {
        return string.Equals(
            role,
            "Agent",
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }


    private static bool IsClaimManager(
        string role)
    {
        return string.Equals(
            role,
            "ClaimManager",
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }


    private static bool IsAdmin(
        string role)
    {
        return string.Equals(
            role,
            "Admin",
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> WithdrawAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid authenticatedUserId,
        string authenticatedRole,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        Claim claim = await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
            claimId,
            cancellationToken);


        EnsureCreator(
            claim,
            authenticatedUserId,
            authenticatedRole);


        bool canWithdraw =
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.Draft ||
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.Submitted;


        if (!canWithdraw)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The claim cannot be withdrawn after review has started.");
        }


        claim.Status = ClaimStatus.Withdrawn;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> AssignToMeAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid claimManagerId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        if (claimManagerId == Guid.Empty)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "A valid Claim Manager ID is required.");
        }


        Claim claim = await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
            claimId,
            cancellationToken);


        bool canBeAssigned =
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.Submitted ||
            claim.Status == ClaimStatus.UnderReview;


        if (!canBeAssigned)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only a submitted or under-review claim can be assigned.");
        }


        if (claim.ClaimManagerId.HasValue &&
            claim.ClaimManagerId.Value != claimManagerId)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The claim is already assigned to another Claim Manager.");
        }


        claim.ClaimManagerId = claimManagerId;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimResponseDto> StartReviewAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid claimManagerId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        if (claimManagerId == Guid.Empty)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "A valid Claim Manager ID is required.");
        }


        Claim claim = await GetRequiredClaimAsync(
            claimId,
            cancellationToken);


        if (claim.Status != ClaimStatus.Submitted)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only a submitted claim can be moved to review.");
        }


        if (claim.ClaimManagerId.HasValue &&
            claim.ClaimManagerId.Value != claimManagerId)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The claim is assigned to another Claim Manager.");
        }


        claim.ClaimManagerId = claimManagerId;
        claim.Status = ClaimStatus.UnderReview;
        claim.ReviewedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        claim.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


        _claimRepository.Update(claim);


        await _claimRepository.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken);


        return MapClaim(claim);
    }
}

using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
//using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IClaimService, ClaimService>();
        services.AddScoped<IClaimFileService, IClaimFileService>();


        return services;
    }
}

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;


public class ClaimCreateDto
{
    [Required]
    public Guid PolicyId { get; set; }


    /*
     * The Policy API response will normally provide the AssetId.
     * However, keeping AssetId in the request makes the selected
     * policy and asset explicit.
     */
    [Required]
    public Guid AssetId { get; set; }


    /*
     * Required only when an Agent creates a claim for a Customer.
     *
     * For a Customer-created claim, the service will resolve
     * CustomerId using the authenticated UserId.
     */
    public Guid? CustomerId { get; set; }


    [Required]
    public DateTime IncidentDate { get; set; }


    [Required]
    [MaxLength(100)]
    public string IncidentType { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    [Required]
    [MaxLength(250)]
    public string IncidentLocation { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    [Required]
    [MaxLength(2000)]
    public string Description { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    [Range(0.01, double.MaxValue)]
    public decimal ClaimedAmount { get; set; }
}

namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;


public class PolicySummaryDto
{
    public Guid PolicyId { get; set; }


    public string PolicyNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    public Guid ProductId { get; set; }


    public Guid QuoteId { get; set; }


    /*
     * Your Policy API returns PropertyId.
     *
     * The Claims domain entity currently stores this value
     * in Claim.AssetId because Claims supports an insured asset.
     */
    public Guid PropertyId { get; set; }


    public Guid CustomerId { get; set; }


    public decimal SumInsured { get; set; }


    public decimal AnnualPremium { get; set; }


    /*
     * Percentage configured on the Policy.
     *
     * Example:
     * 15 means 15 percent.
     */
    public decimal DeductiblePercentage { get; set; }


    public int RenewalPeriodMonths { get; set; }


    public DateTime EffectiveDate { get; set; }


    public DateTime ExpiryDate { get; set; }


    /*
     * Your Policy API serializes the policy status as a number.
     *
     * Based on your current Policy status values:
     * 2 represents Active.
     */
    public int Status { get; set; }


    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }


    /*
     * Add-ons are not required by the current Claims workflow.
     * Keeping the collection allows the complete Policy response
     * to deserialize without requiring Claim API add-on models.
     */
    public List<object> Addons { get; set; } = new();
}

FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain\Entities\Claim.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Enums;
using System.Security.Claims;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Entities;


public class Claim
{
    public Guid ClaimId { get; set; }


    public string ClaimNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    /*
     * These IDs belong to other microservices.
     * Therefore, they are stored as plain Guid values.
     */


    public Guid PolicyId { get; set; }


    public Guid AssetId { get; set; }


    public Guid CustomerId { get; set; }


    /*
     * Auth Service UserId of the Customer or Agent
     * who created the claim.
     */
    public Guid ReportedByUserId { get; set; }


    /*
     * Expected values:
     * Customer
     * Agent
     */
    public string ReportedByRole { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    /*
     * Auth Service UserId of the Claim Manager
     * handling the claim.
     */
    public Guid? ClaimManagerId { get; set; }


    public DateTime IncidentDate { get; set; }


    /*
     * Examples:
     * Earthquake Damage
     * Property Damage
     * Vehicle Damage
     * Contents Damage
     */
    public string IncidentType { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    public string IncidentLocation { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    public string Description { get; set; } = string.Empty;


    /*
     * Amount requested by the Customer or Agent.
     */
    public decimal ClaimedAmount { get; set; }


    /*
     * Loss amount verified by the Claim Manager.
     */
    public decimal? AssessedAmount { get; set; }


    /*
     * Amount approved before the deductible.
     */
    public decimal? ApprovedAmount { get; set; }


    public decimal? DeductibleAmount { get; set; }


    /*
     * FinalSettlementAmount =
     * ApprovedAmount - DeductibleAmount
     */
    public decimal? FinalSettlementAmount { get; set; }


    public ClaimStatus Status { get; set; } = ClaimStatus.Draft;


    /*
     * Claim Manager's explanation for approval,
     * partial approval, or rejection.
     */
    public string? ManagerRemarks { get; set; }


    /*
     * Optional settlement reference entered when
     * the approved claim is marked as settled.
     */
    public string? SettlementReference { get; set; }


    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }


    public DateTime? SubmittedAt { get; set; }


    public DateTime? ReviewedAt { get; set; }


    public DateTime? DecidedAt { get; set; }


    public DateTime? SettledAt { get; set; }


    public DateTime? UpdatedAt { get; set; }


    /*
     * Proof images and PDF documents uploaded
     * for this claim.
     */
    public ICollection<ClaimProof> Proofs { get; set; } = new List<ClaimProof>();
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure\Data\ClaimDbContext.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Reflection.Emit;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.Data;


public class ClaimDbContext : DbContext
{
    public ClaimDbContext(
        DbContextOptions<ClaimDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }


    public DbSet<Claim> Claims => Set<Claim>();


    public DbSet<ClaimProof> ClaimProofs => Set<ClaimProof>();


    protected override void OnModelCreating(
        ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);


        ConfigureClaim(modelBuilder);
        ConfigureClaimProof(modelBuilder);
    }


    private static void ConfigureClaim(
        ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        var entity = modelBuilder.Entity<Claim>();


        entity.ToTable("Claims");


        entity.HasKey(x => x.ClaimId);


        entity.Property(x => x.ClaimId)
            .ValueGeneratedNever();


        entity.Property(x => x.ClaimNumber)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(50);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.ClaimNumber)
            .IsUnique();


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.PolicyId);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.AssetId);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.CustomerId);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.ReportedByUserId);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.ClaimManagerId);


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.Status);


        entity.Property(x => x.ReportedByRole)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(50);


        entity.Property(x => x.IncidentType)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(100);


        entity.Property(x => x.IncidentLocation)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(250);


        entity.Property(x => x.Description)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(2000);


        entity.Property(x => x.ClaimedAmount)
            .HasPrecision(18, 2);


        entity.Property(x => x.AssessedAmount)
            .HasPrecision(18, 2);


        entity.Property(x => x.ApprovedAmount)
            .HasPrecision(18, 2);


        entity.Property(x => x.DeductibleAmount)
            .HasPrecision(18, 2);


        entity.Property(x => x.FinalSettlementAmount)
            .HasPrecision(18, 2);


        entity.Property(x => x.Status)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasConversion<int>();


        entity.Property(x => x.ManagerRemarks)
            .HasMaxLength(2000);


        entity.Property(x => x.SettlementReference)
            .HasMaxLength(100);


        entity.Property(x => x.CreatedAt)
            .IsRequired();


        entity.HasMany(x => x.Proofs)
            .WithOne(x => x.Claim)
            .HasForeignKey(x => x.ClaimId)
            .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
    }


    private static void ConfigureClaimProof(
        ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        var entity = modelBuilder.Entity<ClaimProof>();


        entity.ToTable("ClaimProofs");


        entity.HasKey(x => x.ClaimProofId);


        entity.Property(x => x.ClaimProofId)
            .ValueGeneratedNever();


        entity.HasIndex(x => x.ClaimId);


        entity.Property(x => x.OriginalFileName)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(255);


        entity.Property(x => x.StoredFileName)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(255);


        entity.Property(x => x.FilePath)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(500);


        entity.Property(x => x.ContentType)
            .IsRequired()
            .HasMaxLength(100);


        entity.Property(x => x.Description)
            .HasMaxLength(500);


        entity.Property(x => x.UploadedAt)
            .IsRequired();
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure\FileStorage\ClaimFileService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.FileStorage;


public class ClaimFileService : IClaimFileService
{
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string[]>
        AllowedContentTypes =
            new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            {
                [".jpg"] =
                [
                    "image/jpeg",
                    "image/jpg"
                ],


                [".jpeg"] =
                [
                    "image/jpeg",
                    "image/jpg"
                ],


                [".png"] =
                [
                    "image/png"
                ],


                [".webp"] =
                [
                    "image/webp"
                ],


                [".pdf"] =
                [
                    "application/pdf"
                ]
            };


    private readonly IWebHostEnvironment _environment;
    private readonly ClaimFileStorageOptions _options;


    public ClaimFileService(
        IWebHostEnvironment environment,
        IOptions<ClaimFileStorageOptions> options)
    {
        _environment = environment;
        _options = options.Value;
    }


    public async Task<SavedClaimFile> SaveFileAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        IFormFile file,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ValidateFile(file);


        string extension = Path
            .GetExtension(file.FileName)
            .ToLowerInvariant();


        string storedFileName =
            $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}{extension}";


        string relativeDirectory = Path.Combine(
            "claim-proofs",
            claimId.ToString());


        string relativeFilePath = Path.Combine(
            relativeDirectory,
            storedFileName);


        string webRootPath = GetWebRootPath();


        string absoluteDirectory = Path.Combine(
            webRootPath,
            relativeDirectory);


        Directory.CreateDirectory(absoluteDirectory);


        string absoluteFilePath = Path.Combine(
            absoluteDirectory,
            storedFileName);


        try
        {
            await using FileStream stream = new(
                absoluteFilePath,
                FileMode.CreateNew,
                FileAccess.Write,
                FileShare.None,
                bufferSize: 81920,
                useAsync: true);


            await file.CopyToAsync(
                stream,
                cancellationToken);
        }
        catch
        {
            if (File.Exists(absoluteFilePath))
            {
                File.Delete(absoluteFilePath);
            }


            throw;
        }


        return new SavedClaimFile
        {
            StoredFileName = storedFileName,


            RelativeFilePath = relativeFilePath
                .Replace("\\", "/"),


            ContentType = file.ContentType,


            FileSize = file.Length
        };
    }


    public Task DeleteFileAsync(
        string relativeFilePath,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(relativeFilePath))
        {
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }


        string webRootPath = GetWebRootPath();


        string normalizedRelativePath = relativeFilePath
            .Replace(
                '/',
                Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)
            .TrimStart(
                Path.DirectorySeparatorChar,
                Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar);


        string absoluteFilePath = Path.GetFullPath(
            Path.Combine(
                webRootPath,
                normalizedRelativePath));


        string normalizedWebRoot = Path
            .GetFullPath(webRootPath)
            .TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)
            + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;


        if (!absoluteFilePath.StartsWith(
                normalizedWebRoot,
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The requested proof path is invalid.");
        }


        if (File.Exists(absoluteFilePath))
        {
            File.Delete(absoluteFilePath);
        }


        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }


    private void ValidateFile(IFormFile file)
    {
        if (file is null || file.Length == 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The selected file is empty.");
        }


        long maximumFileSize =
            _options.MaximumFileSizeInMb
            * 1024L
            * 1024L;


        if (file.Length > maximumFileSize)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"Each proof file must be smaller than " +
                $"{_options.MaximumFileSizeInMb} MB.");
        }


        string extension = Path
            .GetExtension(file.FileName)
            .ToLowerInvariant();


        bool extensionIsAllowed =
            _options.AllowedExtensions.Contains(
                extension,
                StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(extension) ||
            !extensionIsAllowed)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Only JPG, JPEG, PNG, WEBP, and PDF files are allowed.");
        }


        if (!AllowedContentTypes.TryGetValue(
                extension,
                out string[]? contentTypes))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The selected file type is not supported.");
        }


        bool contentTypeIsAllowed =
            contentTypes.Contains(
                file.ContentType,
                StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);


        if (!contentTypeIsAllowed)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The file extension does not match its content type.");
        }


        string originalFileName =
            Path.GetFileName(file.FileName);


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(originalFileName))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The uploaded file must have a valid filename.");
        }
    }


    private string GetWebRootPath()
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                _environment.WebRootPath))
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(
                _environment.WebRootPath);


            return _environment.WebRootPath;
        }


        string webRootPath = Path.Combine(
            _environment.ContentRootPath,
            "wwwroot");


        Directory.CreateDirectory(webRootPath);


        return webRootPath;
    }
}

using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text.Json;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;


public class GatewayClient : IGatewayClient
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;


    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions
        JsonOptions = new()
        {
            PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true
        };


    public GatewayClient(HttpClient httpClient)
    {
        _httpClient = httpClient;
    }


    public async Task<CurrentCustomerDto?>
        GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        using HttpResponseMessage response =
            await _httpClient.GetAsync(
                "customers/me",
                cancellationToken);


        if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            return null;
        }


        if (response.StatusCode ==
            HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The Customer Service request was not authenticated.");
        }


        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


        return await response.Content
            .ReadFromJsonAsync<CurrentCustomerDto>(
                JsonOptions,
                cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<PolicySummaryDto?> GetPolicyAsync(
        Guid policyId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        using HttpResponseMessage response =
            await _httpClient.GetAsync(
                $"policies/id/{policyId}",
                cancellationToken);


        if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            return null;
        }


        if (response.StatusCode ==
            HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The Policy Service request was not authenticated.");
        }


        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


        return await response.Content
            .ReadFromJsonAsync<PolicySummaryDto>(
                JsonOptions,
                cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<List<PolicySummaryDto>>
        GetCurrentCustomerPoliciesAsync(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        using HttpResponseMessage response =
            await _httpClient.GetAsync(
                "policies/me",
                cancellationToken);


        if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            return new List<PolicySummaryDto>();
        }


        if (response.StatusCode ==
            HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The Policy Service request was not authenticated.");
        }


        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


        return await response.Content
            .ReadFromJsonAsync<List<PolicySummaryDto>>(
                JsonOptions,
                cancellationToken)
            ?? new List<PolicySummaryDto>();
    }


    public async Task<List<PolicySummaryDto>>
        GetCustomerPoliciesAsync(
            Guid customerId,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        using HttpResponseMessage response =
            await _httpClient.GetAsync(
                $"policies/customer/{customerId}",
                cancellationToken);


        if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            return new List<PolicySummaryDto>();
        }


        if (response.StatusCode ==
            HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The Policy Service request was not authenticated.");
        }


        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


        return await response.Content
            .ReadFromJsonAsync<List<PolicySummaryDto>>(
                JsonOptions,
                cancellationToken)
            ?? new List<PolicySummaryDto>();
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure\HTTPClients\JwtPropagationHandler.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Handlers;


public class JwtPropagationHandler : DelegatingHandler
{
    private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;


    public JwtPropagationHandler(
        IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor)
    {
        _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
    }


    protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(
        HttpRequestMessage request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        string? authorizationHeader =
            _httpContextAccessor
                .HttpContext?
                .Request
                .Headers["Authorization"]
                .FirstOrDefault();


        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                authorizationHeader) &&
            AuthenticationHeaderValue.TryParse(
                authorizationHeader,
                out AuthenticationHeaderValue? parsedHeader))
        {
            request.Headers.Authorization = parsedHeader;
        }


        return await base.SendAsync(
            request,
            cancellationToken);
    }
}

using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;


public class ClaimProofRepository : IClaimProofRepository
{
    private readonly ClaimDbContext _database;


    public ClaimProofRepository(
        ClaimDbContext database)
    {
        _database = database;
    }


    public async Task<List<ClaimProof>> GetByClaimIdAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.ClaimProofs
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(x => x.ClaimId == claimId)
            .OrderByDescending(x => x.UploadedAt)
            .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<ClaimProof?> GetByIdAsync(
        Guid proofId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.ClaimProofs
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(
                x => x.ClaimProofId == proofId,
                cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<int> CountByClaimIdAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.ClaimProofs
            .CountAsync(
                x => x.ClaimId == claimId,
                cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(
        ClaimProof proof,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await _database.ClaimProofs.AddAsync(
            proof,
            cancellationToken);
    }


    public void Delete(ClaimProof proof)
    {
        _database.ClaimProofs.Remove(proof);
    }


    public async Task<bool> SaveChangesAsync(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.SaveChangesAsync(
            cancellationToken) > 0;
    }
}

            .Where(x => x.ReportedByUserId == userId)
            .OrderByDescending(x => x.CreatedAt)
            .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);

            .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<List<Claim>> GetByStatusAsync(

        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.Claims
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(x => x.Proofs)
            .Where(x => x.Status == status)
            .OrderByDescending(x => x.CreatedAt)
            .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<Claim?> GetByIdAsync(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        return await _database.Claims
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(
                x => x.ClaimId == claimId,
                cancellationToken);
    }


    public async Task<Claim?> GetByIdWithProofsAsync(
        Guid claimId,

    {
        return await _database.Claims
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(x => x.Proofs)

    }
