using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Domain.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using System.Security.Claims;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API.Controllers;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/claim-management")]
public class ClaimManagementController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IClaimService _claimService;


    public ClaimManagementController(
        IClaimService claimService)
    {
        _claimService = claimService;
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claim-management/queue
     *
     * Optional:
     * /api/claim-management/queue?status=Submitted
     */
    [HttpGet("queue")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<ClaimResponseDto>>> GetQueue(
        [FromQuery] ClaimStatus? status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out _,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimStatus? selectedStatus =
            status ?? ClaimStatus.Submitted;


        List<ClaimResponseDto> claims =
            await _claimService.GetAllAsync(
                selectedStatus,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claims);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claim-management/all
     *
     * Intended for Claim Managers and Admins.
     */
    [HttpGet("all")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<ClaimResponseDto>>> GetAll(
        [FromQuery] ClaimStatus? status,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out _,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        List<ClaimResponseDto> claims =
            await _claimService.GetAllAsync(
                status,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claims);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claim-management/assigned-to-me
     */
    [HttpGet("assigned-to-me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<ClaimResponseDto>>>
        GetAssignedToMe(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid userId,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        List<ClaimResponseDto> claims =
            await _claimService.GetAssignedToMeAsync(
                userId,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claims);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claim-management/{claimId}
     */
    [HttpGet("{claimId:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> GetById(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto? claim =
            await _claimService.GetByIdAsync(
                claimId,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        if (claim is null)
        {
            return NotFound(new
            {
                Message =
                    $"Claim with ID '{claimId}' was not found."
            });
        }


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * PATCH /api/claim-management/{claimId}/assign-to-me
     */
    [HttpPatch("{claimId:guid}/assign-to-me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> AssignToMe(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid managerId,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.AssignToMeAsync(
                claimId,
                managerId,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * PATCH /api/claim-management/{claimId}/start-review
     */
    [HttpPatch("{claimId:guid}/start-review")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> StartReview(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid managerId,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.StartReviewAsync(
                claimId,
                managerId,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * POST /api/claim-management/{claimId}/review
     *
     * Handles:
     * Approved
     * PartiallyApproved
     * Rejected
     */
    [HttpPost("{claimId:guid}/review")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Review(
        Guid claimId,
        [FromBody] ClaimReviewDto dto,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid managerId,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.ReviewAsync(
                claimId,
                dto,
                managerId,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * PATCH /api/claim-management/{claimId}/settle
     */
    [HttpPatch("{claimId:guid}/settle")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Settle(
        Guid claimId,
        [FromBody] ClaimSettlementDto dto,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetClaimManager(
                out Guid managerId,
                out _,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.SettleAsync(
                claimId,
                dto,
                managerId,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    private bool TryGetClaimManager(
        out Guid userId,
        out string role,
        out ActionResult? error)
    {
        userId = Guid.Empty;
        role = string.Empty;
        error = null;


        string? userIdValue =
            User.FindFirstValue(
                ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);


        if (!Guid.TryParse(
                userIdValue,
                out userId))
        {
            error = Unauthorized(new
            {
                Message =
                    "A valid authenticated user ID was not received from the API Gateway."
            });


            return false;
        }


        role =
            User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.Role)
            ?? string.Empty;


        bool authorized =
            string.Equals(
                role,
                "ClaimManager",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
            string.Equals(
                role,
                "ClaimsManager",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
            string.Equals(
                role,
                "Admin",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);


        if (!authorized)
        {
            error = StatusCode(
                StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
                new
                {
                    Message =
                        "Only a Claim Manager or Admin can access this endpoint."
                });


            return false;
        }


        return true;
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi\Controllers\ClaimsController.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API.DTOs;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using System.Security.Claims;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API.Controllers;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/claims")]
public class ClaimsController : ControllerBase
{
    /*
     * Your Policy API uses numeric statuses.
     * Status 2 represents Active.
     */
    private const int ActivePolicyStatus = 2;


    private readonly IClaimService _claimService;
    private readonly IGatewayClient _gatewayClient;


    public ClaimsController(
        IClaimService claimService,
        IGatewayClient gatewayClient)
    {
        _claimService = claimService;
        _gatewayClient = gatewayClient;
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claims/eligible-policies/me
     *
     * Returns active policies belonging to the
     * authenticated Customer.
     */
    [HttpGet("eligible-policies/me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<PolicySummaryDto>>>
        GetMyEligiblePolicies(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out _,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        if (!IsRole(role, "Customer"))
        {
            return StatusCode(
                StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
                new
                {
                    Message =
                        "Only a Customer can use this endpoint."
                });
        }


        List<PolicySummaryDto> policies =
            await _gatewayClient
                .GetCurrentCustomerPoliciesAsync(
                    cancellationToken);


        List<PolicySummaryDto> activePolicies =
            policies
                .Where(policy =>
                    policy.Status == ActivePolicyStatus)
                .OrderByDescending(policy =>
                    policy.EffectiveDate)
                .ToList();


        return Ok(activePolicies);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claims/eligible-policies/customer/{customerId}
     *
     * Used by an Agent when creating a claim
     * for a Customer.
     */
    [HttpGet(
        "eligible-policies/customer/{customerId:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<PolicySummaryDto>>>
        GetCustomerEligiblePolicies(
            Guid customerId,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out _,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        if (!IsRole(role, "Agent") &&
            !IsRole(role, "Admin"))
        {
            return StatusCode(
                StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
                new
                {
                    Message =
                        "Only an Agent or Admin can access customer policies."
                });
        }


        List<PolicySummaryDto> policies =
            await _gatewayClient
                .GetCustomerPoliciesAsync(
                    customerId,
                    cancellationToken);


        List<PolicySummaryDto> activePolicies =
            policies
                .Where(policy =>
                    policy.Status == ActivePolicyStatus)
                .OrderByDescending(policy =>
                    policy.EffectiveDate)
                .ToList();


        return Ok(activePolicies);
    }


    /*
     * POST /api/claims
     *
     * Creates a claim in Draft status.
     */
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Create(
        [FromBody] ClaimCreateDto dto,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        if (!IsRole(role, "Customer") &&
            !IsRole(role, "Agent"))
        {
            return StatusCode(
                StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
                new
                {
                    Message =
                        "Only a Customer or Agent can create a claim."
                });
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.CreateAsync(
                dto,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return CreatedAtAction(
            nameof(GetById),
            new
            {
                claimId = claim.ClaimId
            },
            claim);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claims/me
     */
    [HttpGet("me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<ClaimResponseDto>>>
        GetMyClaims(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        List<ClaimResponseDto> claims =
            await _claimService.GetMyClaimsAsync(
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claims);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claims/{claimId}
     */
    [HttpGet("{claimId:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> GetById(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto? claim =
            await _claimService.GetByIdAsync(
                claimId,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        if (claim is null)
        {
            return NotFound(new
            {
                Message =
                    $"Claim with ID '{claimId}' was not found."
            });
        }


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * PUT /api/claims/{claimId}
     *
     * Only a Draft claim can be updated.
     */
    [HttpPut("{claimId:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Update(
        Guid claimId,
        [FromBody] ClaimUpdateDto dto,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.UpdateAsync(
                claimId,
                dto,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * POST /api/claims/{claimId}/proofs
     *
     * Request type:
     * multipart/form-data
     *
     * Form fields:
     * files
     * description
     */
    [HttpPost("{claimId:guid}/proofs")]
    [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
    [RequestFormLimits(
        MultipartBodyLengthLimit = 104857600)]
    public async Task<
        ActionResult<List<ClaimProofResponseDto>>>
        UploadProofs(
            Guid claimId,
            [FromForm] UploadClaimProofsDto dto,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        List<ClaimProofResponseDto> proofs =
            await _claimService.UploadProofsAsync(
                claimId,
                dto.Files,
                dto.Description,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(proofs);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/claims/{claimId}/proofs
     */
    [HttpGet("{claimId:guid}/proofs")]
    public async Task<
        ActionResult<List<ClaimProofResponseDto>>>
        GetProofs(
            Guid claimId,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        List<ClaimProofResponseDto> proofs =
            await _claimService.GetProofsAsync(
                claimId,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(proofs);
    }


    /*
     * DELETE /api/claims/{claimId}/proofs/{proofId}
     *
     * Proofs may only be removed while the claim
     * remains in Draft status.
     */
    [HttpDelete(
        "{claimId:guid}/proofs/{proofId:guid}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteProof(
        Guid claimId,
        Guid proofId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        await _claimService.DeleteProofAsync(
            claimId,
            proofId,
            userId,
            role,
            cancellationToken);


        return NoContent();
    }


    /*
     * PATCH /api/claims/{claimId}/submit
     */
    [HttpPatch("{claimId:guid}/submit")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Submit(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.SubmitAsync(
                claimId,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    /*
     * PATCH /api/claims/{claimId}/withdraw
     */
    [HttpPatch("{claimId:guid}/withdraw")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ClaimResponseDto>> Withdraw(
        Guid claimId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        if (!TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
                out Guid userId,
                out string role,
                out ActionResult? error))
        {
            return error!;
        }


        ClaimResponseDto claim =
            await _claimService.WithdrawAsync(
                claimId,
                userId,
                role,
                cancellationToken);


        return Ok(claim);
    }


    private bool TryGetAuthenticatedUser(
        out Guid userId,
        out string role,
        out ActionResult? error)
    {
        userId = Guid.Empty;
        role = string.Empty;
        error = null;


        string? userIdValue =
            User.FindFirstValue(
                ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);


        if (!Guid.TryParse(
                userIdValue,
                out userId))
        {
            error = Unauthorized(new
            {
                Message =
                    "A valid authenticated user ID was not received from the API Gateway."
            });


            return false;
        }


        role =
            User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.Role)
            ?? string.Empty;


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(role))
        {
            error = Unauthorized(new
            {
                Message =
                    "A valid user role was not received from the API Gateway."
            });


            return false;
        }


        return true;
    }


    private static bool IsRole(
        string actualRole,
        string expectedRole)
    {
        return string.Equals(
            actualRole,
            expectedRole,
            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi\Middlewares\ExceptionHandlingMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using System.Net;
using System.Text.Json;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API.Middleware;


public class ExceptionHandlingMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware> _logger;


    public ExceptionHandlingMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException exception)
        {
            await WriteErrorResponseAsync(
                context,
                HttpStatusCode.Forbidden,
                "Access denied",
                exception.Message);
        }
        catch (KeyNotFoundException exception)
        {
            await WriteErrorResponseAsync(
                context,
                HttpStatusCode.NotFound,
                "Resource not found",
                exception.Message);
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException exception)
        {
            await WriteErrorResponseAsync(
                context,
                HttpStatusCode.BadRequest,
                "Invalid operation",
                exception.Message);
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException exception)
        {
            _logger.LogError(
                exception,
                "A downstream service request failed.");


            await WriteErrorResponseAsync(
                context,
                HttpStatusCode.BadGateway,
                "Downstream service error",
                "A required service could not complete the request.");
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
            when (context.RequestAborted.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            _logger.LogInformation(
                "Request was cancelled by the client for path {Path}.",
                context.Request.Path);
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            _logger.LogError(
                exception,
                "An unhandled exception occurred while processing {Method} {Path}.",
                context.Request.Method,
                context.Request.Path);


            await WriteErrorResponseAsync(
                context,
                HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError,
                "Server error",
                "An unexpected error occurred while processing the request.");
        }
    }


    private static async Task WriteErrorResponseAsync(
        HttpContext context,
        HttpStatusCode statusCode,
        string title,
        string detail)
    {
        if (context.Response.HasStarted)
        {
            return;
        }


        context.Response.Clear();
        context.Response.StatusCode = (int)statusCode;
        context.Response.ContentType = "application/problem+json";


        var problem = new ProblemDetails
        {
            Status = (int)statusCode,
            Title = title,
            Detail = detail,
            Instance = context.Request.Path
        };


        problem.Extensions["traceId"] =
            context.TraceIdentifier;


        string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(
            problem,
            new JsonSerializerOptions
            {
                PropertyNamingPolicy =
                    JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase
            });


        await context.Response.WriteAsync(json);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi\Middlewares\OcelotClaimsMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;


public sealed class OcelotClaimsMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> _logger;


    public OcelotClaimsMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        string? userId =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Id"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? username =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Name"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? email =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Email"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? userRole =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Role"].FirstOrDefault();


        _logger.LogInformation(
            "Identity headers received. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Email: {Email}, Role: {Role}",
            userId,
            username,
            email,
            userRole);




        Console.WriteLine("--- Incoming Request Headers ---");
        foreach (var header in context.Request.Headers)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"{header.Key}: {header.Value}");
        }
        Console.WriteLine("--------------------------------");
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
        {
            var claims = new List<Claim>
            {
                new Claim(
                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier,
                    userId)
            };


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Name,
                        username));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Email,
                        email));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userRole))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Role,
                        userRole));
            }


            var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(
                claims,
                authenticationType: "OcelotGatewayAuth",
                nameType: ClaimTypes.Name,
                roleType: ClaimTypes.Role);


            context.User = new ClaimsPrincipal(identity);


            _logger.LogInformation(
                "ClaimsPrincipal created. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Role: {Role}",
                userId,
                username,
                userRole);
        }
        else
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "X-User-Id was not received from the API Gateway. No ClaimsPrincipal was created.");
        }


        await _next(context);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.ClaimsApi\Program.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API.Middleware;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Inteafaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.Data;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.FileStorage;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Handlers;
using Earthquake.ClaimsApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.OpenApi.Models;


namespace Earthquake.ClaimsApi.API;


public class Program
{
    public static async Task Main(string[] args)
    {
        WebApplicationBuilder builder =
            WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


        /*
         * Controllers and API behavior.
         */
        builder.Services.AddControllers();


        builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();


        /*
         * Swagger configuration.
         *
         * Swagger can accept a Bearer token for direct API
         * testing, although normal application traffic should
         * pass through Ocelot.
         */
        builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
        {
            options.SwaggerDoc(
                "v1",
                new OpenApiInfo
                {
                    Title = "Earthquake Claim API",
                    Version = "v1",
                    Description =
                        "Claims processing API for customers, agents, and claim managers."
                });


            options.AddSecurityDefinition(
                "Bearer",
                new OpenApiSecurityScheme
                {
                    Name = "Authorization",
                    Type = SecuritySchemeType.Http,
                    Scheme = "bearer",
                    BearerFormat = "JWT",
                    In = ParameterLocation.Header,
                    Description =
                        "Enter the JWT access token."
                });


            options.AddSecurityRequirement(
                new OpenApiSecurityRequirement
                {
                    {
                        new OpenApiSecurityScheme
                        {
                            Reference =
                                new OpenApiReference
                                {
                                    Type =
                                        ReferenceType.SecurityScheme,
                                    Id = "Bearer"
                                }
                        },
                        Array.Empty<string>()
                    }
                });
        });


        /*
         * SQLite database.
         */
        string connectionString =
            builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString(
                "ClaimDatabase")
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "The ClaimDatabase connection string is missing.");


        builder.Services.AddDbContext<ClaimDbContext>(
            options =>
            {
                options.UseSqlite(connectionString);
            });


        /*
         * Claim file-storage configuration.
         */
        builder.Services.Configure<ClaimFileStorageOptions>(
            builder.Configuration.GetSection(
                ClaimFileStorageOptions.SectionName));


        /*
         * Application services.
         */
        builder.Services.AddScoped<
            IClaimService,
            ClaimService>();


        /*
         * Infrastructure repositories.
         */
        builder.Services.AddScoped<
            IClaimRepository,
            ClaimRepository>();


        builder.Services.AddScoped<
            IClaimProofRepository,
            ClaimProofRepository>();


        /*
         * Local proof-file service.
         */
        builder.Services.AddScoped<
            IClaimFileService,
            ClaimFileService>();


        /*
         * Required for JWT propagation from the current
         * request to internal microservice requests.
         */
        builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();


        builder.Services.AddTransient<
            JwtPropagationHandler>();


        /*
         * Gateway client.
         *
         * The base address should end with /gateway/
         * because GatewayClient uses paths such as:
         *
         * customers/me
         * policies/me
         * policies/id/{policyId}
         */
        string gatewayBaseUrl =
            builder.Configuration["Gateway:BaseUrl"]
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Gateway:BaseUrl is missing.");


        builder.Services
            .AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(
                client =>
                {
                    client.BaseAddress =
                        new Uri(gatewayBaseUrl);


                    client.Timeout =
                        TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
                })
            .AddHttpMessageHandler<
                JwtPropagationHandler>();


        /*
         * CORS for Angular development.
         */
        builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
        {
            options.AddPolicy(
                "AngularDevelopment",
                policy =>
                {
                    policy
                        .WithOrigins(
                            "http://localhost:4200",
                            "https://localhost:4200")
                        .AllowAnyHeader()
                        .AllowAnyMethod();
                });
        });


        WebApplication app = builder.Build();


        /*
         * Ensure the SQLite directory and proof directory exist.
         */
        string dataDirectory = Path.Combine(
            app.Environment.ContentRootPath,
            "Data");


        Directory.CreateDirectory(dataDirectory);


        string webRootDirectory = Path.Combine(
            app.Environment.ContentRootPath,
            "wwwroot");


        Directory.CreateDirectory(webRootDirectory);


        string proofDirectory = Path.Combine(
            webRootDirectory,
            "claim-proofs");


        Directory.CreateDirectory(proofDirectory);


        /*
         * Apply Entity Framework migrations automatically
         * during development.
         *
         * This is acceptable for the current project.
         */
        using (IServiceScope scope =
               app.Services.CreateScope())
        {
            ClaimDbContext database =
                scope.ServiceProvider
                    .GetRequiredService<ClaimDbContext>();


            await database.Database.MigrateAsync();
        }


        /*
         * Middleware order.
         */
        app.UseMiddleware<ExceptionHandlingMiddleware>();


        if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
        {
            app.UseSwagger();
            app.UseSwaggerUI(options =>
            {
                options.SwaggerEndpoint(
                    "/swagger/v1/swagger.json",
                    "Earthquake Claim API v1");


                options.RoutePrefix = "swagger";
            });
        }


        app.UseHttpsRedirection();


        app.UseCors("AngularDevelopment");


        /*
         * Exposes:
         * /claim-proofs/{claimId}/{fileName}
         */
        app.UseStaticFiles();


        /*
         * Rebuild User from the Gateway-provided identity headers.
         */
        app.UseMiddleware<OcelotClaimsMiddleware>();


        app.MapControllers();


        app.MapGet("/", () =>
            Results.Ok(new
            {
                Service = "Earthquake Claim API",
                Status = "Running"
            }));


        await app.RunAsync();
    }
}

        public int TemperatureC { get; set; }


        public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);


        public string? Summary { get; set; }

FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application\Classes\Services\AssetService.cs

using System.Data;
using System.Net.NetworkInformation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Asset;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Mappers;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Asset;
using Earthquake.AssetApi.Application.Mappers;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Customer;
namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Classes.Services;
public class AssetService : IAssetService 
{
    IGatewayClient gwc;
    IAssetRepository assetRepo;
    ICustomerService customerService;
    public AssetService(IGatewayClient gwc, IAssetRepository icr, ICustomerService custServ)
    {
        assetRepo = icr;
        customerService = custServ;
        this.gwc = gwc;
    }


    public List<AssetResponseDto> Fetch()
    {
        List<AssetResponseDto> res = new();
        foreach(Asset cs in assetRepo.GetAll())
        {
            res.Add(AssetMapper.Mapper(cs));
        }
        return res;
    }


    public async Task<AssetFullDetailsDto> Fetch(Guid id)
    {
        Asset cs = assetRepo.Get(id);
        if (cs == null)
        {
            return null;
        }
        Customer cus = await gwc.GetCustomer(cs.CustomerId);
        return AssetMapper.Mapper(cus, cs);
    }


    public async Task<AssetResponseDto> Create(AssetCreateDto assetInp)
    {
        Asset ass = AssetMapper.Mapper(assetInp);
        Customer cs = await gwc.GetCustomer(ass.CustomerId);
        if (ass != null && cs!=null)
        {
            assetRepo.Add(ass);
            return AssetMapper.Mapper(ass);
        }
        Console.WriteLine("Customer exists or asset is null");
        return null;
    }
    public AssetResponseDto Edit(Guid id, AssetUpdateDto assetUpdate)
    {
        Asset asset = assetRepo.Update(id, assetUpdate);
        if (asset != null)
            return AssetMapper.Mapper(asset);
        return null;


    }


    public bool ToggleStatus(Guid id)
    {
        Asset cs = assetRepo.Toggle(id);
        if (cs != null)
        {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }


    public AssetResponseDto Delete(Guid id)
    {
        Asset cs = assetRepo.Delete(id);
        if (cs != null)
        {
            return AssetMapper.Mapper(cs);
        }
        return null;
    }


    public List<Asset> FetchByCustId(Guid id)
    {
        return assetRepo.GetByCustId(id);
    }


    public async Task<List<Asset>> GetAssetsByUserId(Guid userId)
    {
        CustomerFullDetailsDto cust = await customerService.FetchByUserIdAsync(userId);
        return assetRepo.GetByCustId(cust.CustomerId);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application\Classes\Services\CustomerService.cs
================================================================================
using System.Data;
using System.Net.NetworkInformation;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Customer;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Mappers;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using System.Text.Json;
namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Classes.Services;
public class CustomerService : ICustomerService 
{
    IGatewayClient gwc;
    ICustomerRepository custRepo;
    public CustomerService(IGatewayClient gwc, ICustomerRepository icr)
    {
        custRepo = icr;
        this.gwc = gwc;
    }


    public async Task<List<CustomerFullDetailsDto>> FetchAsync()
    {
        List<CustomerFullDetailsDto> res = new();
        List<Customer> customers = custRepo.GetAll();
        List<Guid> userIds = customers.Select(c => c.UserId).Distinct().ToList();
        List<User> users = await gwc.GetUsersBatch(userIds);
        var userMap = users.ToDictionary(u => u.UserId);
        foreach (Customer cs in customers)
        {
            userMap.TryGetValue(cs.UserId, out var user);
            res.Add(CustomerMapper.Mapper(user, cs));
        }
        return res;
    }


    public async Task<CustomerFullDetailsDto> FetchByUserIdAsync(Guid userId)
    {
        CustomerFullDetailsDto res = new CustomerFullDetailsDto();
        Customer customer = custRepo.GetByUserId(userId);
        if (customer == null)
        {
            return null;
        }
        User user = await gwc.GetUser(userId);
        res = CustomerMapper.Mapper(user, customer);
        return res;
    }


    public async Task<CustomerFullDetailsDto> Fetch(Guid id)
    {
        Customer cs = custRepo.Get(id);
        Object user = await gwc.GetUser(cs.UserId);
        if (cs == null)
        {
            return null;
        }
        return CustomerMapper.Mapper(user, cs);
    }


    public async Task<CustomerFullDetailsDto> Create(CustomerCreateDto custInp)
    {
        Customer cs = CustomerMapper.Mapper(custInp);
        Object user = await gwc.GetUser(cs.UserId);
        if (cs != null && user != null)
        {
            custRepo.Add(cs);
            return CustomerMapper.Mapper(user, cs);
        }
        return null;
    }


    public bool ToggleStatus(Guid id)
    {
        Customer cs = custRepo.Toggle(id);
        if (cs != null)
        {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }


    public async Task<CustomerFullDetailsDto> Delete(Guid id)
    {
        Customer cs = custRepo.Delete(id);
        Object user = await gwc.GetUser(id);
        if (cs != null)
        {
            return CustomerMapper.Mapper(user, cs);
        }
        return null;
    }
}

using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
//using Earthquake.CustomerApi.


namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<ICustomerService, CustomerService>();
        services.AddScoped<IAssetService, AssetService>();


        return services;
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application\Mappers\AssetMapper.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Asset;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Enums;
using System.ComponentModel.Design.Serialization;
namespace Earthquake.AssetApi.Application.Mappers;


public class AssetMapper
{
    public static Asset Mapper(AssetCreateDto ccs)
    {
        Asset ass = new Asset();
        ass.AssetId = Guid.NewGuid();
        ass.CustomerId = ccs.CustomerId;
        ass.AssetType = ccs.AssetType;
        ass.AssetName = ccs.AssetName;
        ass.MarketValue = ccs.MarketValue;
        ass.ReplacementValue = ccs.ReplacementValue;


        if (ass.AssetType == CustomerApi.Domain.Enums.AssetType.Vehicle)
        {
            ass.VehicleDetail = ccs.VehicleDetail;
            ass.VehicleDetail.VehicleDetailId = Guid.NewGuid();
            ass.PropertyDetail = null;
        }
        else
        {
            ass.PropertyDetail = ccs.PropertyDetail;
            ass.PropertyDetail.PropertyDetailId = Guid.NewGuid();
            ass.VehicleDetail = null;
        }
        ass.CreatedAt = new DateTime();
        ass.UpdatedAt = new DateTime();
        return ass;
    }


    public static AssetResponseDto Mapper(Asset ass)
    {
        AssetResponseDto ard = new AssetResponseDto();
        ard.AssetId = ass.AssetId;
        ard.CustomerId = ass.CustomerId;
        ard.AssetType = ass.AssetType;
        ard.AssetName = ass.AssetName;
        ard.MarketValue = ass.MarketValue;
        ard.ReplacementValue = ass.ReplacementValue;
        ard.PropertyDetail = ass.PropertyDetail;
        ard.VehicleDetail = ass.VehicleDetail;
        ard.CreatedAt = new DateTime();
        ard.UpdatedAt = new DateTime();
        return ard;
    }
    public static AssetFullDetailsDto Mapper(Customer cus, Asset ass)
    {
        AssetFullDetailsDto afd = new AssetFullDetailsDto();
        afd.AssetId = ass.AssetId;
        afd.CustomerId = ass.CustomerId;
        afd.AssetType = ass.AssetType;
        afd.AssetName = ass.AssetName;
        afd.MarketValue = ass.MarketValue;
        afd.ReplacementValue = ass.ReplacementValue;
        afd.PropertyDetail = ass.PropertyDetail;
        afd.VehicleDetail = ass.VehicleDetail;
        afd.CreatedAt = new DateTime();
        afd.UpdatedAt = new DateTime();
        afd.Customer = cus;
        return afd;
    }




}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application\Mappers\CustomerMapper.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Customer;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Mappers;


public class CustomerMapper
{
    public static Customer Mapper(CustomerCreateDto ccs)
    {
        Customer cs = new Customer();
        cs.UserId = ccs.UserId;
        return cs;
    }


    public static CustomerResponseDto Mapper(Customer cs)
    {
        CustomerResponseDto crs = new CustomerResponseDto();
        crs.CustomerId = cs.CustomerId;
        crs.CustomerSince = cs.CustomerSince;
        crs.NumberOfProperties = cs.NumberOfProperties;
        crs.NumberOfPolicies = cs.NumberOfPolicies;
        crs.UserId = cs.UserId;
        return crs;
    }
    public static CustomerFullDetailsDto Mapper(Object user, Customer cs)
    {
        CustomerFullDetailsDto crs = new CustomerFullDetailsDto();
        crs.CustomerId = cs.CustomerId;
        crs.CustomerSince = cs.CustomerSince;
        crs.NumberOfProperties = cs.NumberOfProperties;
        crs.NumberOfPolicies = cs.NumberOfPolicies;
        crs.User = user;
        crs.IsActive = cs.IsActive;
        return crs;
    }




}

using System;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using System.Security.Permissions;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities
{
    [Index(nameof(CustomerId), IsUnique = true)]
    [Index(nameof(UserId), IsUnique = true)]
    public class Customer
    {
        [Key]
        [DatabaseGenerated(DatabaseGeneratedOption.Identity)]
        public Guid CustomerId { get; set; }


        //[Required]
        //[DatabaseGenerated(DatabaseGeneratedOption.Identity)]
        //public int CustomerNumber { get; set; }


        [Required]
        public Guid UserId { get; set; }


        [Required]
        [Column(TypeName = "date")]
        public DateOnly CustomerSince { get; set; }


        [Required]
        public bool IsActive { get; set; }


        [Required]
        public int NumberOfPolicies { get; set; } = 0;


        [Required]
        public int NumberOfProperties { get; set; } = 0;
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure\Classes\Repository\AssetRepository.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.DTOs.Asset;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;


namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Classses.Repository
{
    public class AssetRepository : IAssetRepository 
    {
        private readonly CustomerDbContext _database;
        public AssetRepository(CustomerDbContext _db) 
        {
            _database = _db;
        }
        public List<Asset> GetAll()
        {
            return _database.Assets
        .Include(a => a.PropertyDetail)     // Joins the Category table
        .Include(a => a.VehicleDetail)     // Joins the Location table
        .ToList();
        }


        public Asset Get(Guid id)
        {
            Asset ass = _database.Assets.Include(a => a.PropertyDetail).Include(a => a.VehicleDetail).FirstOrDefault(c => c.AssetId == id);
            return ass;
        }


        public bool Add(Asset cs)
        {
            if (_database.Assets.FirstOrDefault(c => c.AssetId == cs.AssetId) != null)
            {
                return false;
            }
            _database.Assets.Add(cs);
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return true;
        }


        public Asset Update(Guid id, AssetUpdateDto cs)
        {
            var asset = _database.Assets.Include(a => a.PropertyDetail).Include(a => a.VehicleDetail).FirstOrDefault(x => x.AssetId == id);


            asset.AssetName = cs.AssetName;
            asset.MarketValue = cs.MarketValue;
            asset.ReplacementValue = cs.ReplacementValue;


            if (asset != null)
            {
                if (asset.PropertyDetail != null && cs.PropertyDetail != null)
                {
                    var entry = _database.Entry(asset.PropertyDetail);


                    foreach (var property in entry.Metadata.GetProperties())
                    {
                        if (!property.IsPrimaryKey() && !property.IsForeignKey())
                        {
                            var dtoProp = cs.PropertyDetail.GetType().GetProperty(property.Name);
                            if (dtoProp != null)
                            {
                                entry.Property(property.Name).CurrentValue = dtoProp.GetValue(cs.PropertyDetail);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }


                if (asset.VehicleDetail != null && cs.VehicleDetail != null)
                {
                    var entry = _database.Entry(asset.VehicleDetail);


                    foreach (var property in entry.Metadata.GetProperties())
                    {
                        if (!property.IsPrimaryKey() && !property.IsForeignKey())
                        {
                            var dtoProp = cs.VehicleDetail.GetType().GetProperty(property.Name);
                            if (dtoProp != null)
                            {
                                entry.Property(property.Name).CurrentValue = dtoProp.GetValue(cs.VehicleDetail);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }


                asset.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
                _database.SaveChanges();
            }
            return asset;
        }




        public Asset Delete(Guid id)
        {
            Asset cus = _database.Assets.FirstOrDefault(c => c.AssetId == id);
            if (cus == null)
            {
                return null;
            }
            _database.Assets.Remove(cus);
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return cus;
        }


        public Asset Toggle(Guid id)
        {
            Asset cs = _database.Assets.FirstOrDefault(c => c.AssetId == id);


            if (cs == null)
            {
                return null;
            }
            //cs.IsActive = !cs.IsActive;
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return cs;
        }


        public List<Asset> GetByCustId(Guid id)
        {
            return _database.Assets.Include(a => a.PropertyDetail).Include(a => a.VehicleDetail).Where(ass => ass.CustomerId == id).ToList();
        }
    }


}






================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure\Classes\Repository\CustomerRepository.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;


namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Classses.Repository
{
    public class CustomerRepository : ICustomerRepository
    {
        private readonly CustomerDbContext _database;
        public CustomerRepository(CustomerDbContext _db)
        {
            _database = _db;
        }
        public List<Customer> GetAll()
        {
            return _database.Customers.ToList<Customer>();
        }


        public Customer GetByUserId(Guid userId)
        {
            return _database.Customers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.UserId == userId);
        }


        public Customer Get(Guid id)
        {
            return _database.Customers.FirstOrDefault(c => c.CustomerId == id);
        }


        public bool Add(Customer cs)
        {
            if (_database.Customers.FirstOrDefault(c => c.UserId == cs.UserId) != null)
            {
                return false;
            }
            _database.Customers.Add(cs);
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return true;
        }




        public Customer Delete(Guid id)
        {
            Customer cus = _database.Customers.FirstOrDefault(c => c.CustomerId == id);
            if (cus == null)
            {
                return null;
            }
            _database.Customers.Remove(cus);
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return cus;
        }


        public Customer Toggle(Guid id)
        {
            Customer cs = _database.Customers.FirstOrDefault(c => c.CustomerId == id);


            if (cs == null)
            {
                return null;
            }
            cs.IsActive = !cs.IsActive;
            _database.SaveChanges();
            return cs;
        }
    }


}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Classses.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.Interfaces.Repository;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Http;




public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(
        this IServiceCollection services,
        IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.AddDbContext<CustomerDbContext>(options =>
            options.UseSqlServer(
                configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnectionUrl")));


        services.AddScoped<ICustomerRepository,
                           CustomerRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IAssetRepository,
                           AssetRepository>();


        services.AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(client =>
        {
            client.BaseAddress =
                new Uri("https://localhost:5050/gateway/");
        });


        return services;
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure\HTTPClients\Classes\GatewayClient.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;


namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes
{
    public class GatewayClient : IGatewayClient
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;
        private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;


        public GatewayClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor)
        {
            _httpClient = httpClient;
            _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
        }


        private void AddAuthorizationHeader()
        {
            _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Remove("Authorization");


            var token = _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?
                .Request.Headers["Authorization"]
                .FirstOrDefault();


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token))
            {
                _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization =
                    AuthenticationHeaderValue.Parse(token);
            }
        }


        public async Task<List<Policy>> GetPolicies(Guid id)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            return await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<List<Policy>>
                ($"Policy/customer/{id}")
                ?? new List<Policy>();
        }


        public async Task<Policy?> GetPolicy(int id)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            return await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<Policy>
                ($"Policy/customer/{id}");
        }


        public async Task<User> GetUser(Guid id)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            return await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<User>
                ($"users/{id}");
        }


        public async Task<Customer?> GetCustomer(Guid id)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            return await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<Customer>
                ($"customers/{id}");
        }


        public async Task<List<User>> GetUsersBatch(List<Guid> userIds)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            HttpResponseMessage httpResponse =
                await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("users/batch", userIds);


            httpResponse.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


            return await httpResponse.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<User>>()
                   ?? new List<User>();
        }
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure\Persistence\CustomerDbContext.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.CustomerApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Reflection.Emit;
using System.Security.Claims;
namespace Earthquake.CustomerApi.Infrastructure.Persistence; 


public class CustomerDbContext : DbContext
{
    public CustomerDbContext(DbContextOptions<CustomerDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }


    public DbSet<Customer> Customers { get; set; }


    public DbSet<Asset> Assets { get; set; }


    public DbSet<PropertyDetail> Properties { get; set; }


    public DbSet<VehicleDetail> Vehicles { get; set; }




    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<Customer>()
        .Property(cs => cs.CustomerId)
        .HasDefaultValueSql("NEWSEQUENTIALID()");


        modelBuilder.Entity<Asset>()
               .Property(cs => cs.AssetId)
               .HasDefaultValueSql("NEWSEQUENTIALID()");
        //modelBuilder.Entity<PropertyDetail>()
        //    .Property(cs => cs.PropertyDetailId);
            
    }




    
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application\Services\PolicyService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Domain.Enums;


namespace Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Services;


public class PolicyService : IPolicyService
{
    private readonly IPolicyRepository _repository;
    private readonly IGatewayClient _gatewayClient;


    public PolicyService(
        IPolicyRepository repository,
        IGatewayClient gatewayClient)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _gatewayClient = gatewayClient;
    }


    public async Task<Guid> CreatePolicyAsync(
        CreatePolicyDto dto)
    {
        var policy = new Policy
        {
            PolicyId = Guid.NewGuid(),


            PolicyNumber =
                $"EQ-{DateTime.UtcNow:yyyy}-" +
                $"{Random.Shared.Next(100000, 999999)}",


            QuoteId = dto.QuoteId,


            CustomerId = dto.CustomerId,


            PropertyId = dto.PropertyId,


            SumInsured = dto.SumInsured,


            AnnualPremium = dto.AnnualPremium,


            DeductiblePercentage =
                dto.DeductiblePercentage,


            EffectiveDate = DateTime.UtcNow,


            ExpiryDate =
                DateTime.UtcNow.AddYears(dto.TermYears),


            Status = PolicyStatus.Active,


            CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,


            RenewalPeriodMonths =
                dto.RenewalPeriodMonths
        };


        await _repository.AddAsync(policy);


        await _repository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return policy.PolicyId;
    }


    public async Task<List<Policy>>
        GetCustomerPoliciesAsync(
            Guid customerId)
    {
        return await _repository
            .GetByCustomerAsync(customerId);
    }


    public async Task<List<Policy>> GetMyPoliciesAsync(
        Guid userId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        CustomerSummaryDto? customer =
            await _gatewayClient.GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
                cancellationToken);


        if (customer is null)
        {
            return new List<Policy>();
        }


        /*
         * Additional identity consistency check.
         *
         * The customer endpoint should return the profile belonging
         * to the JWT user making the request.
         */
        if (customer.UserId != Guid.Empty &&
            customer.UserId != userId)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedAccessException(
                "The returned customer profile does not belong " +
                "to the authenticated user.");
        }


        return await _repository
            .GetByCustomerAsync(customer.CustomerId);
    }


    public async Task<Policy?> GetPolicyAsync(
        string policyNumber)
    {
        return await _repository
            .GetByNumberAsync(policyNumber);
    }


    public async Task<Policy?> GetPolicyAsync(
        Guid polId)
    {
        return await _repository
            .GetByIdAsync(polId);
    }


    public async Task ActivatePolicyAsync(
        Guid policyId)
    {
        Policy? policy =
            await _repository.GetByIdAsync(policyId);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                $"Policy '{policyId}' was not found.");
        }


        policy.Status = PolicyStatus.Active;


        await _repository.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task CancelPolicyAsync(
        Guid policyId)
    {
        Policy? policy =
            await _repository.GetByIdAsync(policyId);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            throw new KeyNotFoundException(
                $"Policy '{policyId}' was not found.");
        }


        policy.Status = PolicyStatus.Cancelled;


        await _repository.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task<List<Policy>> GetPoliciesAsync()
    {
        return await _repository.GetAllPolicies();
    }
}

using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Domain.Entities;


namespace Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Services;


public class ProductService : IProductService
{
    private readonly IProductRepository _repository;


    public ProductService(
        IProductRepository repository)
    {
        _repository = repository;
    }


    public async Task<Guid> CreateProductAsync(
        CreateProductDto dto)
    {
        var product = new Product
        {
            ProductId = Guid.NewGuid(),


            ProductCode = dto.ProductCode,


            Name = dto.Name,


            ProductType = dto.ProductType,


            Description = dto.Description,


            MinimumCoverage =
                dto.MinimumCoverage,


            MaximumCoverage =
                dto.MaximumCoverage,


            IsActive = true,


            CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
        };


        await _repository.AddAsync(product);


        await _repository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return product.ProductId;
    }


    public async Task<List<Product>>
        GetProductsAsync()
    {
        return await _repository.GetAllAsync();
    }


    public async Task<Product?>
        GetProductAsync(Guid productId)
    {
        return await _repository
            .GetByIdAsync(productId);
    }
}

public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(
        this IServiceCollection services,
        IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.AddDbContext<PolicyDbContext>(options =>
            options.UseSqlServer(
                configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnectionUrl")));


        services.AddScoped<IProductRepository, ProductRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IPolicyRepository, PolicyRepository>();


        services.AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(client =>
        {
            client.BaseAddress =
                new Uri("https://localhost:5050/gateway/");
        });


        return services;
    }

FILE: Earthquake.PolicyApi.Infrastructure\HTTPClients\Classes\GatewayClient.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;


namespace Earthquake.PolicyApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;


public sealed class GatewayClient : IGatewayClient
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;
    private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;
    private readonly ILogger<GatewayClient> _logger;


    public GatewayClient(
        HttpClient httpClient,
        IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor,
        ILogger<GatewayClient> logger)
    {
        _httpClient = httpClient;
        _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task<CustomerSummaryDto?> GetCurrentCustomerAsync(
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(
            HttpMethod.Get,
            "customers/me");


        AddAuthorizationHeader(request);


        _logger.LogInformation(
            "Requesting the current customer from {RequestUri}",
            request.RequestUri);


        using HttpResponseMessage response =
            await _httpClient.SendAsync(
                request,
                cancellationToken);


        if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "No customer profile was found for the current user.");


            return null;
        }


        string responseBody =
            await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(
                cancellationToken);


        if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
        {
            _logger.LogError(
                "Getting current customer failed. Status: {StatusCode}. Response: {ResponseBody}",
                response.StatusCode,
                responseBody);


            throw new HttpRequestException(
                $"Getting the current customer failed. " +
                $"Status: {(int)response.StatusCode} " +
                $"{response.StatusCode}. " +
                $"Response: {responseBody}");
        }


        return await response.Content
            .ReadFromJsonAsync<CustomerSummaryDto>(
                cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
    }


    private void AddAuthorizationHeader(
        HttpRequestMessage request)
    {
        string? authorizationHeader = _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?.Request.Headers["Authorization"].FirstOrDefault();


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(authorizationHeader))
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "The incoming request does not contain an Authorization header.");


            return;
        }


        if (AuthenticationHeaderValue.TryParse(
                authorizationHeader,
                out AuthenticationHeaderValue? parsedHeader))
        {
            request.Headers.Authorization = parsedHeader;
        }
        else
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "The incoming Authorization header could not be parsed.");
        }
    }
}

using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.PolicyApi.Infrastructure.Repositories;
public class PolicyRepository : IPolicyRepository
{
    private readonly PolicyDbContext _context;


    public PolicyRepository(PolicyDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(Policy policy)
    {
        await _context.Policies.AddAsync(policy);
    }


    public async Task<Policy?> GetByIdAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _context.Policies.Include(x => x.Addons).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.PolicyId == id);
    }


    public async Task<Policy?> GetByNumberAsync(string number)
    {
        return await _context.Policies.Include(x => x.Addons).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.PolicyNumber == number);
    }


    public async Task<List<Policy>>GetByCustomerAsync(Guid customerId)
    {
        return await _context.Policies.Where(x => x.CustomerId == customerId).ToListAsync();
    }


    public async Task<List<Policy>> GetAllPolicies()
    {
        return await _context.Policies.ToListAsync();
    }


    public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
    {
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using System.Security.Claims;


namespace Earthquake.PolicyService.API.Controllers;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/policies")]
public class PolicyController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IPolicyService _policyService;


    public PolicyController(
        IPolicyService policyService)
    {
        _policyService = policyService;
    }


    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<Policy>>>
        GetPolicies()
    {
        List<Policy> policies =
            await _policyService.GetPoliciesAsync();


        return Ok(policies);
    }


    [HttpGet("id/{polId:guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<Policy>>
        GetPolicyById(Guid polId)
    {
        Policy policy =
            await _policyService.GetPolicyAsync(polId);


        Console.WriteLine("Sending:", policy);


        return Ok(policy);
    }


    /*
     * GET /api/policies/me
     *
     * Returns policies belonging to the currently
     * authenticated customer.
     */
    [Authorize]
    [HttpGet("me")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<Policy>>>
        GetMyPolicies(
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        string? userIdValue =
            User.FindFirstValue(
                ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);


        if (!Guid.TryParse(
                userIdValue,
                out Guid userId))
        {
            return Unauthorized(new
            {
                Message =
                    "A valid user ID was not received " +
                    "from the API Gateway."
            });
        }


        try
        {
            List<Policy> policies =
                await _policyService.GetMyPoliciesAsync(
                    userId,
                    cancellationToken);


            return Ok(policies);
        }
        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException exception)
        {
            return Unauthorized(new
            {
                Message = exception.Message
            });
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException exception)
        {
            return StatusCode(
                StatusCodes.Status502BadGateway,
                new
                {
                    Message =
                        "The customer service could not be reached.",
                    Details = exception.Message
                });
        }
    }


    [HttpGet("{policyNumber}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetPolicy(
        string policyNumber)
    {
        Policy? policy =
            await _policyService.GetPolicyAsync(
                policyNumber);


        if (policy is null)
        {
            return NotFound(new
            {
                Message =
                    $"Policy '{policyNumber}' was not found."
            });
        }


        return Ok(policy);
    }


    [HttpGet("customer/{customerId:guid}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetCustomerPolicies(
        Guid customerId)
    {
        List<Policy> policies =
            await _policyService
                .GetCustomerPoliciesAsync(
                    customerId);


        return Ok(policies);
    }


    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreatePolicy(
        [FromBody] CreatePolicyDto dto)
    {
        Guid policyId =
            await _policyService.CreatePolicyAsync(dto);


        return Ok(new
        {
            PolicyId = policyId,
            Message = "Policy created successfully"
        });
    }


    [HttpPatch("{policyId:guid}/activate")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ActivatePolicy(
        Guid policyId)
    {
        try
        {
            await _policyService
                .ActivatePolicyAsync(policyId);


            return Ok(new
            {
                Message = "Policy activated"
            });
        }
        catch (KeyNotFoundException exception)
        {
            return NotFound(new
            {
                Message = exception.Message
            });
        }
    }


    [HttpPatch("{policyId:guid}/cancel")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CancelPolicy(
        Guid policyId)
    {
        try
        {
            await _policyService
                .CancelPolicyAsync(policyId);


            return Ok(new
            {
                Message = "Policy cancelled"
            });
        }
        catch (KeyNotFoundException exception)
        {
            return NotFound(new
            {
                Message = exception.Message
            });
        }
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.PolicyApi\Middlewares\OcelotClaimsMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;


public sealed class OcelotClaimsMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> _logger;


    public OcelotClaimsMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        string? userId =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Id"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? username =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Name"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? email =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Email"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? userRole =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Role"].FirstOrDefault();


        _logger.LogInformation(
            "Identity headers received. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Email: {Email}, Role: {Role}",
            userId,
            username,
            email,
            userRole);


        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
        {
            var claims = new List<Claim>
            {
                new Claim(
                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier,
                    userId)
            };


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Name,
                        username));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Email,
                        email));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userRole))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Role,
                        userRole));
            }


            var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(
                claims,
                authenticationType: "OcelotGatewayAuth",
                nameType: ClaimTypes.Name,
                roleType: ClaimTypes.Role);


            context.User = new ClaimsPrincipal(identity);


            _logger.LogInformation(
                "ClaimsPrincipal created. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Role: {Role}",
                userId,
                username,
                userRole);
        }
        else
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "X-User-Id was not received from the API Gateway. No ClaimsPrincipal was created.");
        }


        await _next(context);
    }
}

using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Application;
using Earthquake.PolicyApi.Infrastructure;


var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


builder.Services.AddControllers();


builder.Services.AddAuthentication();
builder.Services.AddAuthorization();


builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();


builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer(); // 1. REQUIRED for routing discovery
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();


var app = builder.Build();


// 2. REQUIRED: Enable the middleware to serve generated Swagger JSON and UI
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
    {
        c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Customer API V1");
    });
}




app.UseMiddleware<OcelotClaimsMiddleware>();


app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();


app.MapControllers();


app.Run();
