================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Classes\Services\GenerateQuoteService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class GenerateQuoteService : IGenerateQuoteService
{


    private readonly IRiskAssessmentService _riskAssessmentService;


    public GenerateQuoteService(
        IRiskAssessmentService riskAssessmentService)
    {
        _riskAssessmentService = riskAssessmentService;
    }
    public GeneratedQuoteResponseDto GenerateQuoteByUwr(GenerateQuoteRequestDto request)
    {
        decimal hazardScore = CalculateHazardScore(request);


        decimal vulnerabilityScore =
            CalculateVulnerabilityScore(request);


        decimal exposureScore =
            CalculateExposureScore(request);


        decimal ari =
            hazardScore +
            vulnerabilityScore +
            exposureScore;


        string riskCategory =
            GetRiskCategory(ari);


        decimal coveragePercentage =
            GetCoveragePercentage(riskCategory);


        decimal sumInsured =
            request.PropertyValue *
            coveragePercentage /
            100;


        decimal premiumRate =
            GetPremiumRate(riskCategory);


        decimal premium =
            sumInsured *
            premiumRate /
            100;


        premium = ApplyAddons(
            premium,
            request);


        return new GeneratedQuoteResponseDto
        {
            HazardScore = Math.Round(hazardScore, 2),
            VulnerabilityScore = Math.Round(vulnerabilityScore, 2),
            ExposureScore = Math.Round(exposureScore, 2),
            ActuarialRiskIndex = Math.Round(ari, 2),
            RiskCategory = riskCategory,
            CoveragePercentage = coveragePercentage,
            SumInsured = sumInsured,
            PremiumRate = premiumRate,
            AnnualPremium = premium,
            DeductiblePercentage =
                GetDeductible(riskCategory)
        };
    }


    private decimal CalculateHazardScore(GenerateQuoteRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal zone =
            r.SeismicZone switch
            {
                "VeryLow" => 10,
                "Low" => 25,
                "Moderate" => 50,
                "High" => 75,
                "VeryHigh" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal frequency =
            r.HistoricalEarthquakes switch
            {
                <= 5 => 10,
                <= 20 => 30,
                <= 50 => 60,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal magnitude =
            r.MaxMagnitude switch
            {
                < 4 => 10,
                < 5 => 30,
                < 6 => 60,
                < 7 => 80,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal distance =
            r.DistanceFromFaultKm switch
            {
                > 200 => 10,
                > 100 => 40,
                > 50 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (zone * 0.30m) +
            (frequency * 0.15m) +
            (magnitude * 0.10m) +
            (distance * 0.05m);
    }


    private decimal CalculateVulnerabilityScore(GenerateQuoteRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal material =
            r.ConstructionType switch
            {
                "ReinforcedConcrete" => 20,
                "Steel" => 30,
                "Brick" => 60,
                "Masonry" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal age =
            r.BuildingAge switch
            {
                < 10 => 20,
                < 25 => 40,
                < 50 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal floors =
            r.Floors switch
            {
                <= 2 => 20,
                <= 5 => 40,
                <= 10 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal usage =
            r.Usage switch
            {
                "Residential" => 30,
                "Apartment" => 50,
                "Commercial" => 70,
                "Industrial" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        return
            (material * 0.12m) +
            (age * 0.08m) +
            (floors * 0.05m) +
            (usage * 0.05m);
    }


    private decimal CalculateExposureScore(GenerateQuoteRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal valueScore =
            r.PropertyValue switch
            {
                < 1000000 => 20,
                < 5000000 => 40,
                < 10000000 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal claimScore =
            r.PreviousClaims switch
            {
                0 => 10,
                1 => 40,
                <= 3 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (valueScore * 0.05m) +
            (claimScore * 0.05m);
    }


    private string GetRiskCategory(decimal ari)
    {
        if (ari <= 25)
            return "Low";


        if (ari <= 50)
            return "Moderate";


        if (ari <= 75)
            return "High";


        return "Critical";
    }


    private decimal GetCoveragePercentage(string category)
    {
        return category switch
        {
            "Low" => 100,
            "Moderate" => 95,
            "High" => 90,
            _ => 80
        };
    }


    private decimal GetPremiumRate(string category)
    {
        decimal baseRate = 0.25m;


        decimal loading =
            category switch
            {
                "Low" => 0.50m,
                "Moderate" => 0.80m,
                "High" => 1.20m,
                _ => 1.80m
            };


        return baseRate + loading;
    }


    private decimal GetDeductible(string category)
    {
        return category switch
        {
            "Low" => 2,
            "Moderate" => 5,
            "High" => 10,
            _ => 15
        };
    }


    private decimal ApplyAddons(decimal premium,GenerateQuoteRequestDto request)
    {
        if (request.ContentsCoverage)
            premium += premium * 0.08m;


        if (request.TemporaryAccommodation)
            premium += premium * 0.05m;


        if (request.VehicleCoverage)
            premium += premium * 0.07m;


        return Math.Round(premium, 2);
    }
}




================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Classes\Services\QuoteDraftService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs.QuoteDraft;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Enums;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class QuoteDraftService : IQuoteDraftService
{
    private readonly IQuoteRepository _quoteRepository;


    public QuoteDraftService(IQuoteRepository quoteRepository)
    {
        _quoteRepository = quoteRepository;
    }


    public async Task<List<Guid>> SaveQuoteDraftsAsync(SaveQuoteDraftRequestDto request)
    {
        List<Guid> quoteIds = new();


        foreach (var dto in request.Quotes)
        {
            var quoteId = Guid.NewGuid();


            var quote = new Quote
            {
                QuoteId = quoteId,


                QuoteRequestId = request.QuoteRequestId,


                PropertyId = request.PropertyId,


                QuoteReferenceNumber = GenerateQuoteReference(dto.CoverageType),


                Status = QuoteStatus.Generated,


                CoverageType = dto.CoverageType,


                CoveragePercentage = dto.CoveragePercentage,


                CoveragePeriodYears = request.CoveragePeriodYears,


                RenewalPeriodMonths = request.RenewalPeriodMonths,


                SumInsured = dto.SumInsured,


                PremiumRate = dto.PremiumRate,


                BasePremium = dto.AnnualPremium,


                AnnualPremium = dto.AnnualPremium,


                TotalPremium = dto.AnnualPremium * request.CoveragePeriodYears,


                DeductiblePercentage = dto.DeductiblePercentage,


                RiskScore = dto.RiskScore,


                RiskCategory = dto.RiskCategory,


                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,


                ValidUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(30),


                IsSelected = false,


                RequestedBy = request.RequestedBy,
            };


            await _quoteRepository.AddAsync(quote);


            quoteIds.Add(quoteId);
        }


        await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return quoteIds;
    }


    private string GenerateQuoteReference(string coverageType)
    {
        return $"QT-{coverageType[..3].ToUpper()}-{DateTime.UtcNow.Ticks}";
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Classes\Services\QuoteRecommendationService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs.QuoteRecommendation;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class QuoteRecommendationService
    : IQuoteRecommendationService
{
    public List<RecommendedQuoteDto> GenerateQuotes(
        GenerateQuoteOptionsRequestDto request)
    {
        return new List<RecommendedQuoteDto>
        {
            CreateQuote(
                "Basic",
                80,
                1.0m,
                15,
                request),


            CreateQuote(
                "Standard",
                90,
                1.2m,
                10,
                request),


            CreateQuote(
                "Premium",
                100,
                1.5m,
                5,
                request)
        };
    }


    private RecommendedQuoteDto CreateQuote(
        string packageName,
        decimal coveragePercentage,
        decimal packageMultiplier,
        decimal deductiblePercentage,
        GenerateQuoteOptionsRequestDto request)
    {
        decimal sumInsured =
            request.PropertyValue *
            coveragePercentage / 100;


        decimal baseRate =
            GetBaseRate(request.RiskCategory);


        decimal premiumRate =
            baseRate * packageMultiplier;


        decimal annualPremium =
            sumInsured *
            premiumRate /
            100;


        annualPremium =
            ApplyAddons(
                annualPremium,
                request);


        return new RecommendedQuoteDto
        {
            PackageName = packageName,


            CoveragePercentage = coveragePercentage,


            SumInsured =
                Math.Round(sumInsured, 2),


            PremiumRate =
                Math.Round(premiumRate, 2),


            AnnualPremium =
                Math.Round(annualPremium, 2),


            DeductiblePercentage =
                deductiblePercentage,


            RiskCategory =
                request.RiskCategory,


            RiskScore =
                request.RiskScore
        };
    }


    private decimal GetBaseRate(
        string riskCategory)
    {
        return riskCategory switch
        {
            "Low" => 0.75m,
            "Moderate" => 1.05m,
            "High" => 1.45m,
            "Critical" => 2.05m,
            _ => 1.00m
        };
    }


    private decimal ApplyAddons(
        decimal premium,
        GenerateQuoteOptionsRequestDto request)
    {
        if (request.ContentsCoverage)
        {
            premium += premium * 0.08m;
        }


        if (request.TemporaryAccommodation)
        {
            premium += premium * 0.05m;
        }


        if (request.VehicleCoverage)
        {
            premium += premium * 0.07m;
        }


        return premium;
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Classes\Services\QuoteService.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Contracts;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Mappers;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Enums;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class QuoteService : IQuoteService
{
    private readonly IQuoteRequestRepository _quoteRequestRepository;


    private readonly IQuoteRepository _quoteRepository;


    private readonly IGatewayClient _gatewayClient;


    public QuoteService(IQuoteRequestRepository quoteRequestRepository, IQuoteRepository quoteRepository, IGatewayClient gwclient)
    {
        _quoteRequestRepository = quoteRequestRepository;
        _quoteRepository = quoteRepository;
        _gatewayClient = gwclient;
    }
    public async Task<List<Quote>> GetQuotesAsync()
    {
        return await _quoteRepository.GetAll();
    }
    public async Task<List<Quote>> GetQuotesAsync(Guid requestId)
    {
        return await _quoteRepository.GetByRequestIdAsync(requestId);
    }


    public async Task<List<QuoteRequest>> GetQuoteRequestsAsync(){
        return await _quoteRequestRepository.GetAllAsync();
    }


    public async Task<Guid> CreateRequestAsync(CreateQuoteRequestDto dto)
    {
        QuoteRequest request = QuoteMapper.Mapper(dto);


        await _quoteRequestRepository.AddAsync(request);


        await _quoteRequestRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        return request.QuoteRequestId;
    }


    public async Task GenerateQuotesAsync(Guid requestId, GenerateQuotesDto dto)
    {
        QuoteRequest request = await _quoteRequestRepository.GetByIdAsync(requestId);


        if (request == null)
            throw new Exception($"Quote Request {requestId} not found.");


        if (!dto.Quotes.Any())
            throw new Exception("At least one quote is required.");


        foreach (CreateQuoteDto quoteDto in dto.Quotes)
        {
            Quote quote = QuoteMapper.Mapper(requestId, request.AssetId, quoteDto);
            
            await _quoteRepository.AddAsync(quote);
        }


        request.Status = QuoteStatus.Generated;


        await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        await _quoteRequestRepository.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task<Quote> GetQuoteAsync(Guid quoteId)
    {
        return await _quoteRepository.GetByIdAsync(quoteId);
    }
    public async Task SelectQuoteAsync(Guid quoteId)
    {
        Quote quote = await _quoteRepository.GetByIdAsync(quoteId);


        if (quote == null)
            throw new Exception("Quote not found.");


        List<Quote> allQuotes = await _quoteRepository.GetByRequestIdAsync(quote.QuoteRequestId);


        foreach (var existing in allQuotes)
        {
            existing.IsSelected = false;


            await _quoteRepository.UpdateAsync(existing);
        }


        quote.IsSelected = true;


        quote.Status = QuoteStatus.CustomerSelected;


        await _quoteRepository.UpdateAsync(quote);


        await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task ApproveQuoteAsync(Guid quoteId)
    {
        var quote = await _quoteRepository.GetByIdAsync(quoteId);
        var quotes = await _quoteRepository.GetByRequestIdAsync(quote.QuoteRequestId);


        if (quote == null)
            throw new Exception("Quote not found.");


        if (!quote.IsSelected)
            throw new Exception("Quote must be selected before approval.");


        foreach( var q in quotes)
        {
            q.Status = QuoteStatus.CustomerNotSelected;
        }
        
        quote.Status = QuoteStatus.Approved;


        await _quoteRepository.UpdateAsync(quote);


        await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();


        await ConvertToPolicyAsync(quoteId);
    }


    public async Task RejectQuoteAsync(Guid quoteId,string reason)
    {
        var quote = await _quoteRepository.GetByIdAsync(quoteId);


        if (quote == null)
            throw new Exception("Quote not found.");


        quote.Status = QuoteStatus.Rejected;


        await _quoteRepository.UpdateAsync(quote);


        await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task ConvertToPolicyAsync(Guid quoteId)
    {
        Quote quote = await _quoteRepository.GetByIdAsync(quoteId);


        if (quote == null)
            throw new Exception("Quote not found.");


        if (quote.Status != QuoteStatus.Approved)
        {
            throw new Exception("Only approved quotes can be converted.");
        }


        quote.Status = QuoteStatus.ConvertedToPolicy;


        //Post a request to create a policy 
        Object property = await _gatewayClient.GetProperty(quote.PropertyId);
        if (property is System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonProperty)
        {
            Guid customerId = jsonProperty.GetProperty("customerId").GetGuid();
            Console.WriteLine($"Extracted Customer ID: {customerId}");
            PolicyDto policy = QuotePolicyMapper.Mapper(customerId, quote);
            bool success = await _gatewayClient.AddPolicy(policy);
            if (success)
            {
                await _quoteRepository.UpdateAsync(quote);


                await _quoteRepository.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
        }
        else
        {
            throw new Exception("Couldnot convert the Quote into Policy.");
        }


    }


    public async Task<List<Quote>> GetQuotesByCustIdAsync(Guid customerId)
    {
        return await _quoteRepository.GetByCustIdAsync(customerId);
    }




    public async Task<QuoteRequest?> GetQuoteRequestAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _quoteRequestRepository.GetByIdAsync(id);
    }


    public async Task<List<QuoteRequest>> GetQuoteRequestsByCustIdAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _quoteRequestRepository.GetQuoteRequestsByCustIdAsync(id);
    }




}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Classes\Services\RiskAssessmentService.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs.RiskAssessment;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;


public class RiskAssessmentService : IRiskAssessmentService
{
    public RiskAssessmentResponseDto AssessPropertyRisk(
        PropertyRiskAssessmentRequestDto request)
    {
        decimal hazard =
            CalculatePropertyHazardScore(request);


        decimal vulnerability =
            CalculatePropertyVulnerabilityScore(request);


        decimal exposure =
            CalculatePropertyExposureScore(request);


        decimal total =
            hazard +
            vulnerability +
            exposure;


        return new RiskAssessmentResponseDto
        {
            HazardScore = Math.Round(hazard, 2),
            VulnerabilityScore = Math.Round(vulnerability, 2),
            ExposureScore = Math.Round(exposure, 2),
            TotalRiskScore = Math.Round(total, 2),
            RiskCategory = GetRiskCategory(total),
            Recommendation = GetRecommendation(total)
        };
    }


    public RiskAssessmentResponseDto AssessVehicleRisk(
        VehicleRiskAssessmentRequestDto request)
    {
        decimal hazard =
            CalculateVehicleHazardScore(request);


        decimal vulnerability =
            CalculateVehicleVulnerabilityScore(request);


        decimal exposure =
            CalculateVehicleExposureScore(request);


        decimal total =
            hazard +
            vulnerability +
            exposure;


        return new RiskAssessmentResponseDto
        {
            HazardScore = Math.Round(hazard, 2),
            VulnerabilityScore = Math.Round(vulnerability, 2),
            ExposureScore = Math.Round(exposure, 2),
            TotalRiskScore = Math.Round(total, 2),
            RiskCategory = GetRiskCategory(total),
            Recommendation = GetRecommendation(total)
        };
    }


    private string GetRiskCategory(decimal score)
    {
        if (score <= 25)
            return "Low";


        if (score <= 50)
            return "Moderate";


        if (score <= 75)
            return "High";


        return "Critical";
    }


    private string GetRecommendation(decimal score)
    {
        if (score <= 50)
            return "Recommended";


        if (score <= 75)
            return "Underwriter Review Required";


        return "Senior Underwriter Review Required";
    }




    private decimal CalculatePropertyHazardScore(
        PropertyRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal zone =
            r.SeismicZone switch
            {
                "VeryLow" => 10,
                "Low" => 25,
                "Moderate" => 50,
                "High" => 75,
                "VeryHigh" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal frequency =
            r.HistoricalEarthquakes switch
            {
                <= 5 => 10,
                <= 20 => 30,
                <= 50 => 60,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal magnitude =
            r.MaxMagnitude switch
            {
                < 4 => 10,
                < 5 => 30,
                < 6 => 60,
                < 7 => 80,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal distance =
            r.DistanceFromFaultKm switch
            {
                > 200 => 10,
                > 100 => 40,
                > 50 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (zone * 0.30m) +
            (frequency * 0.15m) +
            (magnitude * 0.10m) +
            (distance * 0.05m);
    }


    private decimal CalculatePropertyVulnerabilityScore(
        PropertyRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal material =
            r.ConstructionType switch
            {
                "ReinforcedConcrete" => 20,
                "Steel" => 30,
                "Brick" => 60,
                "Masonry" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal age =
            r.BuildingAge switch
            {
                < 10 => 20,
                < 25 => 40,
                < 50 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal floors =
            r.Floors switch
            {
                <= 2 => 20,
                <= 5 => 40,
                <= 10 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal usage =
            r.Usage switch
            {
                "Residential" => 30,
                "Apartment" => 50,
                "Commercial" => 70,
                "Industrial" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        return
            (material * 0.12m) +
            (age * 0.08m) +
            (floors * 0.05m) +
            (usage * 0.05m);
    }


    private decimal CalculatePropertyExposureScore(
        PropertyRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal value =
            r.PropertyValue switch
            {
                < 1000000 => 20,
                < 5000000 => 40,
                < 10000000 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal claims =
            r.PreviousClaims switch
            {
                0 => 10,
                1 => 40,
                <= 3 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (value * 0.05m) +
            (claims * 0.05m);
    }




    private decimal CalculateVehicleHazardScore(
        VehicleRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal zone =
            r.SeismicZone switch
            {
                "VeryLow" => 10,
                "Low" => 25,
                "Moderate" => 50,
                "High" => 75,
                "VeryHigh" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal frequency =
            r.HistoricalEarthquakes switch
            {
                <= 5 => 10,
                <= 20 => 30,
                <= 50 => 60,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal distance =
            r.DistanceFromFaultKm switch
            {
                > 200 => 10,
                > 100 => 40,
                > 50 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (zone * 0.35m) +
            (frequency * 0.15m) +
            (distance * 0.10m);
    }


    private decimal CalculateVehicleVulnerabilityScore(
        VehicleRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal age =
            r.VehicleAge switch
            {
                < 3 => 20,
                < 7 => 40,
                < 12 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal type =
            r.VehicleType switch
            {
                "Motorcycle" => 30,
                "Sedan" => 50,
                "SUV" => 70,
                "Luxury" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        decimal parking =
            r.ParkingType switch
            {
                "Garage" => 20,
                "Covered" => 40,
                "Open" => 100,
                _ => 50
            };


        return
            (age * 0.10m) +
            (type * 0.10m) +
            (parking * 0.10m);
    }


    private decimal CalculateVehicleExposureScore(
        VehicleRiskAssessmentRequestDto r)
    {
        decimal value =
            r.VehicleValue switch
            {
                < 500000 => 20,
                < 1500000 => 50,
                < 3000000 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal claims =
            r.PreviousClaims switch
            {
                0 => 10,
                1 => 40,
                <= 3 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        decimal mileage =
            r.AnnualMileage switch
            {
                < 10000 => 20,
                < 20000 => 50,
                < 30000 => 70,
                _ => 100
            };


        return
            (value * 0.10m) +
            (claims * 0.10m) +
            (mileage * 0.10m);
    }


}

using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Classes.Services;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
//using Earthquake.QuoteApi.


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application;


public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddApplication(
        this IServiceCollection services)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IQuoteService, QuoteService>();
        services.AddScoped<IGenerateQuoteService, GenerateQuoteService>();
        services.AddScoped<IRiskAssessmentService,RiskAssessmentService>();
        services.AddScoped<IQuoteRecommendationService,QuoteRecommendationService>();
        services.AddScoped<IQuoteDraftService, QuoteDraftService>();


        return services;
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Mappers\QuoteMapper.cs
================================================================================
using Azure.Core;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Enums;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Mappers
{
    public static class QuoteMapper
    {
        public static QuoteRequest Mapper(CreateQuoteRequestDto dto)
        {
            QuoteRequest request = new QuoteRequest();


            request.QuoteRequestId = Guid.NewGuid();


            request.AssetId = dto.PropertyId;


            request.RequestedBy = dto.RequestedBy;


            request.RequestedCoverage = dto.RequestedCoverage;


            request.RequestedPremium = dto.RequestedPremium;


            request.RequestedPeriodYears = dto.RequestedPeriodYears;


            request.Remarks = dto.Remarks;


            request.RequestedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;


            request.Status = QuoteStatus.Requested;
           
            return request;
        }


        public static Quote Mapper(Guid requestId, Guid PropertyId,  CreateQuoteDto quoteDto)
        {
            Quote quote = new Quote
            {
                QuoteId = Guid.NewGuid(),


                QuoteRequestId = requestId,


                PropertyId = PropertyId,


                QuoteReferenceNumber = GenerateQuoteNumber(),


                Status = QuoteStatus.Generated,


                CoverageType =
                   quoteDto.CoverageType,


                CoveragePeriodYears =
                   quoteDto.CoveragePeriodYears,


                RenewalPeriodMonths =
                   quoteDto.RenewalPeriodMonths,


                SumInsured =
                   quoteDto.SumInsured,


                AnnualPremium =
                   quoteDto.AnnualPremium,


                TotalPremium =
                   quoteDto.AnnualPremium,


                DeductiblePercentage =
                   quoteDto.DeductiblePercentage,


                CreatedAt =
                   DateTime.UtcNow,


                ValidUntil =
                   DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(30),


                Addons =
                   quoteDto.Addons.Select(addon =>
                       new QuoteAddon
                       {
                           QuoteAddonId =
                               Guid.NewGuid(),


                           Name = addon,


                           PremiumImpact = 0
                       })
                   .ToList()
            };


            return quote;
        }


        private static string GenerateQuoteNumber()
        {
            return $"QT-{DateTime.UtcNow:yyyy}-{Random.Shared.Next(100000, 999999)}";
        }
    }
}




================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application\Mappers\QuotePolicyMapper.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Contracts;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Mappers;
public static class QuotePolicyMapper
{
    public static PolicyDto Mapper(Guid custId, Quote quote) 
    {
        PolicyDto policy = new PolicyDto();
        policy.QuoteId = quote.QuoteId;
        policy.CustomerId = custId;
        policy.PropertyId = quote.PropertyId;
        policy.SumInsured = quote.SumInsured;
        policy.AnnualPremium = quote.AnnualPremium;
        policy.DeductiblePercentage = quote.DeductiblePercentage;
        policy.TermYears = quote.CoveragePeriodYears;
        policy.Addons = quote.Addons.Select(x => x.Name).ToList();
        policy.RenewalPeriodMonths = quote.RenewalPeriodMonths;
        return policy;
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure\Classes\Repositories\QuoteRepository.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Identity.Client;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.Classes.Repositories;
public class QuoteRepository : IQuoteRepository
{
    private readonly QuoteDbContext _context;


    public QuoteRepository(
        QuoteDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(Quote quote)
    {
        await _context.Quotes.AddAsync(quote);
    }


    public async Task<Quote> GetByIdAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _context.Quotes.Include(x => x.Addons).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.QuoteId == id);
    }


    public async Task<List<Quote>>
        GetByRequestIdAsync(Guid requestId)
    {
        return await _context.Quotes.Where(x => x.QuoteRequestId == requestId).Include(x => x.Addons).ToListAsync();
    }


    public Task UpdateAsync(Quote quote)
    {
        _context.Quotes.Update(quote);


        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
    public async Task<List<Quote>> GetAll()
    {
        return await _context.Quotes.ToListAsync<Quote>();
    }




    public async Task<List<Quote>> GetByCustIdAsync(Guid custId)
    {
        return await _context.Quotes.Where(x => x.RequestedBy == custId).Include(x => x.Addons).ToListAsync();
    }
    public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
    {
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


}

using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;

namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.Classes.Repositories;
public class QuoteRequestRepository : IQuoteRequestRepository
{
    private readonly QuoteDbContext _context;


    public QuoteRequestRepository(
        QuoteDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }


    public async Task AddAsync(QuoteRequest request)
    {
        await _context.QuoteRequests.AddAsync(request);
    }


    public async Task<QuoteRequest?>
        GetByIdAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _context.QuoteRequests
            .Include(x => x.Quotes)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(
                x => x.QuoteRequestId == id);
    }


    public async Task<List<QuoteRequest>>
        GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await _context.QuoteRequests
            .ToListAsync();
    }




    public async Task SaveChangesAsync()
    {
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
    }


    public async Task<List<QuoteRequest>?> GetQuoteRequestsByCustIdAsync(Guid id)
    {
        return await _context.QuoteRequests.Where(x => x.RequestedBy == id).ToListAsync();
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure\DependencyInjection.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Http;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Repositories;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.Classes.Repositories;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes;




public static class DependencyInjection
{
    public static IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(this IServiceCollection services,IConfiguration configuration)
    {
        services.AddScoped<IQuoteRepository,QuoteRepository>();
        services.AddScoped<IQuoteRequestRepository, QuoteRequestRepository>();
        services.AddDbContext<QuoteDbContext>(options =>
            options.UseSqlServer(
                configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnectionUrl")));




        services.AddHttpClient<IGatewayClient, GatewayClient>(client =>
        {
            client.BaseAddress =
                new Uri("https://localhost:5050/gateway/");
        });


        return services;
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure\HTTPClients\Classes\GatewayClient.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Contracts;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.HTTPClients.Interfaces;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure.HTTPClients.Classes
{
    public class GatewayClient : IGatewayClient
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;
        private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;


        public GatewayClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor)
        {
            _httpClient = httpClient;
            _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
        }


        private void AddAuthorizationHeader()
        {
            _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Remove("Authorization");


            var token = _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?
                .Request.Headers["Authorization"]
                .FirstOrDefault();


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token))
            {
                _httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization =
                    AuthenticationHeaderValue.Parse(token);
            }
        }


        public async Task<bool> AddPolicy(PolicyDto quote)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            var response = await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync(
                "policies",
                quote);
            Console.WriteLine("Request sent to Add Policy", response.IsSuccessStatusCode);


            return response.IsSuccessStatusCode;
        }


        public async Task<object?> GetProperty(Guid propertyId)
        {
            AddAuthorizationHeader();


            var re = await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<object>(
                $"assets/{propertyId}");


            Console.WriteLine(re);
            return re;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi\Controllers\QuoteController.cs
================================================================================
using Azure.Core;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/quotes")]
public class QuoteController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IQuoteService _quoteService;
    private readonly IGenerateQuoteService _generateQuoteService;


    public QuoteController(IQuoteService quoteService, IGenerateQuoteService genserv)
    {
        _quoteService = quoteService;
        _generateQuoteService = genserv;
    }


    //Request Controllers


    [HttpGet("requests")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<QuoteRequest>>> GetQuoteRequests()
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuoteRequestsAsync());
    }


    [HttpGet("requests/{id:Guid}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<QuoteRequest>> GetQuoteRequest(Guid id)
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuoteRequestAsync(id));
    }


    [HttpPost("requests")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreateRequest(CreateQuoteRequestDto dto)
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.CreateRequestAsync(dto));
    }


    [HttpPost("requests/{requestId}/generate")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Generate(Guid requestId, GenerateQuotesDto dto)
    {
        await _quoteService.GenerateQuotesAsync(requestId, dto);


        return Ok();
    }


    [HttpGet("requests/customer/{customerId}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<QuoteRequest>>> GetQuoteRequestsByCustId(Guid customerId)
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuoteRequestsByCustIdAsync(customerId));
    }


    [HttpGet("requests/{requestId}/quotes")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<Quote>>> GetQuotes(Guid requestId)
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuotesAsync(requestId));
    }








    //Quote Controllers


    [HttpGet("")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<Quote>>> GetQuotes()
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuotesAsync());
    }


    [HttpPost("generate")]
    public ActionResult<GeneratedQuoteResponseDto> GenerateQuote(GenerateQuoteRequestDto request)
    {
        var result = _generateQuoteService.GenerateQuoteByUwr(request);


        return Ok(result);
    }


    [HttpGet("{quoteId}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<Quote>> GetByQuouteId(Guid quoteId)
    {
        return Ok(await _quoteService.GetQuoteAsync(quoteId));
    }


    [HttpPost("{quoteId}/select")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Select(Guid quoteId)
    {
        await _quoteService.SelectQuoteAsync(quoteId);


        return Ok();
    }


    [HttpPost("{quoteId}/approve")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Approve(Guid quoteId)
    {
        await _quoteService.ApproveQuoteAsync(quoteId);


        return Ok();
    }


    [HttpPost("{quoteId}/reject")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Reject(Guid quoteId,string reason)
    {
        await _quoteService.RejectQuoteAsync(quoteId,reason);


        return Ok();
    }


    [HttpPost("{quoteId}/convert-to-policy")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Convert(Guid quoteId)
    {
        await _quoteService.ConvertToPolicyAsync(quoteId);


        return Ok();
    }
}

using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs.QuoteDraft;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Presentation.Controllers;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/quote-drafts")]
public class QuoteDraftController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IQuoteDraftService _quoteDraftService;


    public QuoteDraftController(IQuoteDraftService quoteDraftService){
        _quoteDraftService = quoteDraftService;
    }


    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> SaveQuotes(SaveQuoteDraftRequestDto request)
    {
        var result = await _quoteDraftService.SaveQuoteDraftsAsync(request);


        return Ok(result);
    }
}

    public QuoteRecommendationController(IQuoteRecommendationService quoteRecommendationService){
        _quoteRecommendationService = quoteRecommendationService;
    }

        return Ok(result);
    }
}

using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.DTOs.RiskAssessment;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application.Interfaces.Services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


namespace Earthquake.QuoteApi.Presentation.Controllers;


[ApiController]
[Route("api/risk-assessment")]
public class RiskAssessmentController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IRiskAssessmentService _riskAssessmentService;


    public RiskAssessmentController(IRiskAssessmentService riskAssessmentService)
    {
        _riskAssessmentService = riskAssessmentService;
    }


    [HttpPost("property")]
    public IActionResult AssessPropertyRisk(PropertyRiskAssessmentRequestDto request)
    {
        var response = _riskAssessmentService.AssessPropertyRisk(request);


        return Ok(response);
    }


    [HttpPost("vehicle")]
    public IActionResult AssessVehicleRisk(VehicleRiskAssessmentRequestDto request)
    {
        var response = _riskAssessmentService.AssessVehicleRisk(request);


        return Ok(response);
    }
}

FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi\Middlewares\OcelotClaimsMiddleware.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;


public sealed class OcelotClaimsMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> _logger;


    public OcelotClaimsMiddleware(
        RequestDelegate next,
        ILogger<OcelotClaimsMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }


    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        string? userId =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Id"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? username =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Name"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? email =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Email"].FirstOrDefault();


        string? userRole =
            context.Request.Headers["X-User-Role"].FirstOrDefault();


        _logger.LogInformation(
            "Identity headers received. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Email: {Email}, Role: {Role}",
            userId,
            username,
            email,
            userRole);


        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId))
        {
            var claims = new List<Claim>
            {
                new Claim(
                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier,
                    userId)
            };


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Name,
                        username));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Email,
                        email));
            }


            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userRole))
            {
                claims.Add(
                    new Claim(
                        ClaimTypes.Role,
                        userRole));
            }


            var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(
                claims,
                authenticationType: "OcelotGatewayAuth",
                nameType: ClaimTypes.Name,
                roleType: ClaimTypes.Role);


            context.User = new ClaimsPrincipal(identity);


            _logger.LogInformation(
                "ClaimsPrincipal created. UserId: {UserId}, UserName: {UserName}, Role: {Role}",
                userId,
                username,
                userRole);
        }
        else
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(
                "X-User-Id was not received from the API Gateway. No ClaimsPrincipal was created.");
        }


        await _next(context);
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: Earthquake.QuoteApi\Program.cs
================================================================================
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Application;
using Earthquake.QuoteApi.Infrastructure;


var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


builder.Services.AddControllers();


builder.Services.AddAuthentication();
builder.Services.AddAuthorization();


builder.Services.AddApplication();
builder.Services.AddInfrastructure(builder.Configuration);
builder.Services.AddHttpContextAccessor();


builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer(); // 1. REQUIRED for routing discovery
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();


var app = builder.Build();


// 2. REQUIRED: Enable the middleware to serve generated Swagger JSON and UI
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
    {
        c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Customer API V1");
    });
}




app.UseMiddleware<OcelotClaimsMiddleware>();


app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();


app.MapControllers();


app.Run();

namespace QuoteApi
{
    public class WeatherForecast
    {
        public DateOnly Date { get; set; }


        public int TemperatureC { get; set; }


        public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);


        public string? Summary { get; set; }
    }
}

FILE: OcelotGateway\Controllers\WeatherForecastController.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;


namespace OcelotApiGateway.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("[controller]")]
    public class WeatherForecastController : ControllerBase
    {
        private static readonly string[] Summaries = new[]
        {
            "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
        };


        private readonly ILogger<WeatherForecastController> _logger;


        public WeatherForecastController(ILogger<WeatherForecastController> logger)
        {
            _logger = logger;
        }


        [HttpGet(Name = "GetWeatherForecast")]
        public IEnumerable<WeatherForecast> Get()
        {
            return Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index => new WeatherForecast
            {
                Date = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                TemperatureC = Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                Summary = Summaries[Random.Shared.Next(Summaries.Length)]
            })
            .ToArray();
        }
    }
}

FILE: OcelotGateway\Program.cs
================================================================================
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using MMLib.SwaggerForOcelot.DependencyInjection;
using MMLib.SwaggerForOcelot.Middleware;
using Ocelot.DependencyInjection;
using Ocelot.Middleware;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text;


namespace OcelotApiGateway
{
    public class Program
    {
        public static async Task Main(string[] args)
        {
            var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);


            const string customScheme =
                "GatewayAuthenticationSchemeKey";


            const string keyStr =
                "Earthquake_SuperSecure_JwtSecretKey_AtLeast32BytesLong_2026!";


            /*
             * Load Ocelot configuration before registering Ocelot.
             */
            builder.Configuration.AddJsonFile(
                "ocelot.json",
                optional: false,
                reloadOnChange: true);


            /*
             * Configure JWT authentication.
             */
            builder.Services
                .AddAuthentication(options =>
                {
                    options.DefaultAuthenticateScheme =
                        customScheme;


                    options.DefaultChallengeScheme =
                        customScheme;
                })
                .AddJwtBearer(customScheme, options =>
                {
                    /*
                     * Do not automatically map JWT claim names.
                     *
                     * Your current token already contains the full
                     * ClaimTypes URIs, so we will create short aliases
                     * after token validation.
                     */
                    options.MapInboundClaims = false;


                    options.SaveToken = true;


                    options.TokenValidationParameters =
                        new TokenValidationParameters
                        {
                            ValidateIssuer = true,
                            ValidateAudience = true,
                            ValidateLifetime = true,
                            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,


                            ValidIssuer =
                                "Earthquake.AuthApi",


                            ValidAudience =
                                "Earthquake",


                            IssuerSigningKey =
                                new SymmetricSecurityKey(
                                    Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(
                                        keyStr)),


                            ClockSkew =
                                TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2),


                            /*
                             * These identify which claims represent
                             * the user's name and role.
                             */
                            NameClaimType =
                                ClaimTypes.Name,


                            RoleClaimType =
                                ClaimTypes.Role
                        };


                    options.Events = new JwtBearerEvents
                    {
                        OnMessageReceived = context =>
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "Authorization header received: " +
                                context.Request.Headers
                                    .ContainsKey("Authorization"));


                            return Task.CompletedTask;
                        },


                        OnTokenValidated = context =>
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "\n=== JWT TOKEN VALIDATED ===");


                            if (context.Principal?.Identity
                                is not ClaimsIdentity identity)
                            {
                                Console.WriteLine(
                                    "ClaimsIdentity was not available.");


                                return Task.CompletedTask;
                            }


                            /*
                             * Read the original full URI claims.
                             */
                            string? userId =
                                identity.FindFirst(
                                    ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)
                                ?.Value;


                            string? email =
                                identity.FindFirst(
                                    ClaimTypes.Email)
                                ?.Value;


                            string? userName =
                                identity.FindFirst(
                                    ClaimTypes.Name)
                                ?.Value;


                            string? userRole =
                                identity.FindFirst(
                                    ClaimTypes.Role)
                                ?.Value;


                            /*
                             * Add short alias claims for Ocelot.
                             *
                             * Ocelot can now use:
                             * user_id
                             * user_email
                             * user_name
                             * user_role
                             */
                            AddClaimIfMissing(
                                identity,
                                "user_id",
                                userId);


                            AddClaimIfMissing(
                                identity,
                                "user_email",
                                email);


                            AddClaimIfMissing(
                                identity,
                                "user_name",
                                userName);


                            AddClaimIfMissing(
                                identity,
                                "user_role",
                                userRole);


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "=== CLAIMS AVAILABLE TO OCELOT ===");


                            foreach (var claim in identity.Claims)
                            {
                                Console.WriteLine(
                                    $"Claim Type: '{claim.Type}' " +
                                    $"--> Value: '{claim.Value}'");
                            }


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "=================================\n");


                            return Task.CompletedTask;
                        },


                        OnAuthenticationFailed = context =>
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "Token verification failed!");


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                $"Reason: {context.Exception.Message}");


                            return Task.CompletedTask;
                        },


                        OnChallenge = context =>
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "JWT authentication challenge.");


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                $"Error: {context.Error}");


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                $"Description: " +
                                $"{context.ErrorDescription}");


                            return Task.CompletedTask;
                        }
                    };
                });


            /*
             * Register Ocelot.
             */
            builder.Services.AddOcelot(
                builder.Configuration);


            builder.Services.AddSwaggerForOcelot(
                builder.Configuration);


            builder.Services.AddControllers();
            builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
            builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();


            /*
             * CORS configuration.
             *
             * Both allowed origins should be provided in the same
             * WithOrigins call.
             */
            builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
            {
                options.AddPolicy("AllowFE", policy =>
                {
                    policy
                        .WithOrigins(
                            "http://localhost:4200",
                            "https://anirudh.vishwavasu.com")
                        .AllowAnyHeader()
                        .AllowAnyMethod()
                        .AllowCredentials();
                });
            });


            var app = builder.Build();


            if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
            {
                app.UseSwagger();
                app.UseSwaggerUI();
            }


            /*
             * Log requests received by the gateway.
             */
            app.Use(async (context, next) =>
            {
                Console.WriteLine(
                    $"Gateway received: " +
                    $"{context.Request.Method} " +
                    $"{context.Request.Path} " +
                    $"{context.TraceIdentifier}");


                await next();
            });


            app.UseSwaggerForOcelotUI(options =>
            {
                options.PathToSwaggerGenerator =
                    "/swagger/docs";
            });


            app.UseHttpsRedirection();


            app.UseCors("AllowFE");


            /*
             * Authentication must execute before Ocelot.
             */
            app.UseAuthentication();
            app.UseAuthorization();


            app.MapControllers();


            /*
             * Optional Ocelot-specific claim debugging.
             *
             * OnTokenValidated already prints the claims, but this
             * confirms that Ocelot can also see the alias claims.
             */
            var ocelotPipelineConfiguration =
                new OcelotPipelineConfiguration
                {
                    PreAuthorizationMiddleware =
                        async (context, next) =>
                        {
                            ClaimsPrincipal user =
                                context.User;


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "\n=== OCELOT PIPELINE CLAIMS ===");


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                $"Authenticated: " +
                                $"{user.Identity?.IsAuthenticated}");


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                $"Authentication type: " +
                                $"{user.Identity?.AuthenticationType}");


                            if (user.Identity?.IsAuthenticated
                                == true)
                            {
                                foreach (var claim in user.Claims)
                                {
                                    Console.WriteLine(
                                        $"Claim Type: " +
                                        $"'{claim.Type}' " +
                                        $"--> Value: " +
                                        $"'{claim.Value}'");
                                }
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                Console.WriteLine(
                                    "Ocelot did not receive an " +
                                    "authenticated principal.");
                            }


                            Console.WriteLine(
                                "==============================\n");


                            await next.Invoke();
                        }
                };


            /*
             * Ocelot is the terminal gateway middleware.
             */
            await app.UseOcelot(
                ocelotPipelineConfiguration);


            app.Run();
        }


        private static void AddClaimIfMissing(
            ClaimsIdentity identity,
            string claimType,
            string? claimValue)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(claimValue))
            {
                return;
            }


            if (identity.HasClaim(
                    claim => claim.Type == claimType))
            {
                return;
            }


            identity.AddClaim(
                new Claim(
                    claimType,
                    claimValue));
        }
    }
}

      "sslPort": 44320
    }

  "profiles": {

      "commandName": "Project",
      "dotnetRunMessages": true,
      "launchBrowser": true,
      "launchUrl": "swagger",
      "applicationUrl": "https://localhost:5050",
      "environmentVariables": {
        "ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT": "Development"
      }
    }
  }

FILE: OcelotGateway\WeatherForecast.cs

namespace OcelotApiGateway
{
    public class WeatherForecast
    {
        public DateOnly Date { get; set; }


        public int TemperatureC { get; set; }


        public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);


        public string? Summary { get; set; }
    }
}
